(A) Nominal-price audit (Yahoo prices are split-adjusted; nominal = adjusted x later split ratios; 2,039 of 30,080 flips have a later split). Newly eligible (adjusted >= $5, really < $5): train n87 mean -2.7%, BIG 5.7%; test n147 mean -10.8%, median -12.3%, win 9%, BIG 1.4%. Drop-outs n21 | 28. Corrected sub-$5 universe: train n576 mean +29.8% (was +36.9%), BIG 13.2% (14.3%); test n1,472 mean +5.6% (was +7.3%), median -7.7%, BIG 8.3% (8.9%).
(B) Survivorship: 0 of 1,656 names end before Oct 2026: the wide universe holds survivors only (delisted names absent; unquantified upward bias).
(C) Full 40-day windows only (54 late flips dropped): +30/-15 target / stop / timeout: train 27.5 / 41.6 / 31.0%, test 24.3 / 45.7 / 30.0%, late 2026 24.9 / 52.5 / 22.6%.
(D) ASY high vs low (matched cells): target-first 29.0 vs 25.8% | 26.2 vs 22.6% | 32.1 vs 18.0%; stop-first 38.9 vs 44.5 | 44.5 vs 46.9 | 51.9 vs 53.2; actual-exit return difference t -0.1 | +1.1 | +0.9 (test ex-best3 +5.7% vs +3.1%). Still a weak lead; does not reach actual-exit returns with significance.
(E) PLACEBO, sub-$5 daily flips vs 5 matched random days of the same ticker (+-250d, close < $5): +30/-15 target 27.5 vs 27.2% (train), 24.3 vs 24.8% (test), 24.9 vs 26.8% (late); 40-day return 11.9 vs 11.1%, 6.7 vs 7.2%, -0.2 vs +4.8% (t -2.1). Random days in the vector BEAR state: 40d +14.2% | +8.7% | +5.1%; BULL state +6.9% | +4.9% | +4.4%. On this survivor-only sample the sub-$5 daily flip shows NO entry edge over random days; the returns belong to the universe (and partly to survivorship). Flips beat random bull-state days (t +2.0 train, ATR-label z +2.3 test) = entering at the flip beats entering later in the same state.
215 LARGE CAPS (54 cached S&P names, 17,241 live-universe flips vs 5 random RTH bars of the same ticker within +-20 trading days; day-clustered t): 15m track 20-day forward return flip 2.55% vs random 1.51% (train, +1.04pp, t +2.4), 1.54% vs 0.61% (test, +0.93pp, t +3.4); 5d +0.49pp | +0.25pp (t 1.3 | 0.8). 1H track: +0.46pp (t 0.1) train, -0.29pp (t -2.6) test: no entry edge. The 15m large-cap flip has a real entry edge; the 1H large-cap track and the sub-$5 daily flip do not show one.

## Report 224: pure randomness on the Lucid 50K eval math (xs/report_224_random_eval_convexity.py, report_224b_chart.py -> scratchpad coinflip_eval_convexity.png)
No strategy: one coin-flip trade a day on real NQ 5m bars 2018-01..2026-07 (2,090 days), entry 09:35, stop/target or 15:55 close, cost 1 pt, fixed MNQ size; 3,000 random paths per cell; rules as in the fleet harness (EOD-trailing $2,000 MLL locking at +$100, $3,000 target with the 50% best-day rule, 5 x $150 days per payout, min(50% balance, $2,000) x 90%, 5 payouts max, caps 40 eval / 20-30-40 funded, fee $88; no monthly or activation fees).
Mean per random trade after cost: stop 50/target 50 -1.07 pts, 50/100 +0.45, 50/150 +0.90, hold to close -1.00, 100/25 -2.48 (wide-target geometries are slightly positive at random = intraday trend/fat tails; not significant).
Eval pass rate: peaks 24% (50/50 at 8 MNQ), 31% (50/150 at 1 MNQ, 180 days; 28% at 5 MNQ in 11 days), 22% (hold to close at 3 MNQ); falls to 5-16% at 40 MNQ.
Funded expected total payout per account: peaks $669 (50/50 at 10 MNQ), $705-727 (50/150 at 5, 50/100 at 8), $634 (hold at 3); share paid at least once is highest at the smallest size that can make a $150 day (52-63%) and falls to 5-7% at 40 MNQ.
Net per eval, same size both phases: 50/50 peak +$58 at 10 MNQ (negative below 5 and above 20 MNQ); 50/150 peak +$109 at 5 MNQ; hold to close +$50 at 3 MNQ. Best split cell +$137 (eval 50/150 at 1 MNQ, funded 50/100 at 8 MNQ). MC error about +-$15.
Read: the payout structure is convex enough that zero-edge trading is about break-even to slightly positive per eval at the right size, with the peak where one loss is roughly 15-40% of the drawdown (stop 50 pts x 5-10 MNQ = $500-$1,000 vs the $2,000 MLL); oversizing (30-40 MNQ) and undersizing (cannot make a $150 day) both lose the fee.

## Reports 225 / 225b / 225c / 225d: payout-convexity levers on the real algo, Lucid 50K sim (xs/report_225_convexity_levers.py, _225b_combos.py, _225c_chain.py, _225d_chain_more.py)
Baseline (COMBO without FIG-V, eval E2, funded F0, payout taken as soon as it is >= $500), 2018-21 | 2022-25 starts: pass 94% | 82%, paid once 78% | 61%, payouts/eval 1.80 | 2.06, net $2,082 | $3,119 per eval; one account at a time 2018-26: 24 evals, 51 payouts, net $8,971 a year, 0.53 payouts a month.
SIZE MAP (both phases): x0.5 pass 100 | 97, paid 96 | 92, net $2,255 | $3,502; x0.75 $1,809 | $3,227; x1.25 $2,019 | $2,094; x1.5 $1,167 | $2,015; x2 $1,490 | $911. Eval-only x0.5: pass 100 | 97, paid 92 | 74, net $2,634 | $4,060 (pass day 96 | 46 vs 58 | 27); eval x1.5 / x2 worse. Funded-only x0.5 $1,803 | $2,611, x1.5 $2,602 | $2,570, x2 $2,297 | $1,915. Bigger is worse everywhere; the algo sits at or above the peak size.
EVAL POLICY when behind: E0 flat $1,952 | $2,288; PRESS x1.5 below .5 cushion $2,271 | $2,166 (pass 79 | 66); BARBELL $1,937 | $2,959; INVERSE $2,292 | $2,108. Pressing near the floor loses in 2022-25: with a real edge, de-risking (E2) keeps the option alive. E2 stays.
FUNDED: F1 clip(c,.4,1) paid 84 | 78, net $2,215 | $3,311; PROTECT at $4,000 no change; POSTPAY $2,350 | $3,154.
PAYOUT TIMING: take a payout only when >= $1,000: net $3,196 | $3,214 (paid 74 | 59); only when >= $2,000 (the full payout): payouts/eval 3.33 | 2.19, net $5,912 | $3,847, paid once 72 | 52 (lower), first payout day 116 | 50. Mechanism: a small first payout fixes the floor at +$100 and leaves a tiny cushion; waiting for $4,000 leaves ~$1,900 of cushion after the payout.
DAY LOCKS: funded day lock +$150 / +$300 cuts net to $751 | $1,928 and $1,165 | $2,041 (bad); eval day cap +$1,500 $1,961 | $3,321; daily loss stop $500 $2,399 | $2,345, $1,500 $2,082 | $3,458.
COMBOS (chosen after seeing both eras): K2 eval x0.5 + payout >= $2,000 + funded F1: pass 100 | 97, paid 94 | 78, payouts/eval 4.57 | 3.39, net $8,135 | $6,015 per eval, first payout day 152 | 72. K1 (no F1) $7,182 | $5,068, paid 89 | 66. K4 (payout >= $1,000) $4,727 | $4,384, paid 96 | 88. K5 eval x0.75 + payout >= $2,000 $6,538 | $4,538.
PER UNIT TIME (one account at a time 2018-26, single path): baseline $8,971 a year / 0.53 payouts a month; payout >= $2,000 only $12,422 / 0.58 (17 evals); + daily stop $1,500 $11,532 / 0.54; K5 $11,071 / 0.52; payout >= $2,000 + F1 $10,878 / 0.51; eval x0.75 + payout >= $2,000 + F1 $10,642 / 0.50; payout >= $1,000 $9,391 / 0.52; K2 $8,649 / 0.41 (12 evals); F1 only $7,464 / 0.58; eval x0.5 only $7,172 / 0.42. Halving eval size raises per-eval return but not money per month; the payout threshold raises both.
Assumes the trader may defer a payout request until the balance reaches $4,000; not verified against Lucid's current rules. No monthly fees modeled.

## Report 226: Rey's deployment targets, measured not fitted (xs/report_226_targets_fleet.py) - Nov 17 2026
TARGETS (stated by Rey; do NOT rebuild the system to fit them): prop algo on a 50K: dream 4 payouts a month, longest gap between payouts <= 1 month; want 2 a month, longest gap about a month and some days, <= $200 spent inside the longest drought; MINIMUM to deploy 1 payout a month, <= 2.5 months between payouts, <= $250 spent inside the longest drought. Penny screener: minimum acceptable about where it is; want > 50% of signals above +30%. Large-cap screener: just below the want, above the minimum.
WHERE THE PROP ALGO IS (one account at a time, backtest 2018-26): baseline 0.53 payouts a month, longest gap 305 days (2019-03-11 -> 2020-01-10; then 197 d Mar-Sep 2024, 196 d Mar-Oct 2021, 183 d Jul 2022-Jan 2023), most fees inside one gap $264, 58% of months with no payout; payouts by year 2018:2 2019:3 2020:6 2021:3 2022:7 2023:6 2024:9 2025:15. Full-payout rule (>= $2,000): 0.58 a month, longest gap 197 days, fees inside one gap $176, 53% of months with no payout; by year 2019:5 2020:5 2021:6 2022:8 2023:9 2024:8 2025:15.
MORE ACCOUNTS (started 15 trading days apart, same algo): payouts a month scale (2 accounts 1.05-1.17, 4 accounts 2.13-2.34, 5 accounts 2.68-2.92) but the longest gap does not shrink (305 -> 266 days baseline; 197 -> 184 full payout) and fees inside a gap multiply ($528 at 2 accounts baseline): accounts running the same trades fail and pay on the same days. Frequency is reachable with accounts; the drought length is the binding gap vs the 75-day minimum.

## Reports 227-228: small-target trades on alignment, and 1m/5m vector flips (xs/report_227_small_target_alignment.py, report_228_ltf_vector_flips.py)
NQ 2018-01..2026-07, cost 1.0 pt, train 2018-21 | test 2022-26, day-clustered t.
227 (context = 15m vector, or 15m + 1H vectors aligned; triggers = 5m EMA20 pullback, 6-bar breakout; placebos = every bar in the context, coin-flip direction): fixed 10-pt target loses everywhere: 10/10 stop -1.5 to -1.9 pts train, -2.8 to -3.1 test (win 39-47%, t -9 to -40); 10/20 -1.2 to -1.9 | -2.4 to -3.2. ATR-scaled exits are near break-even: aligned EMA pullback 1xATR/2xATR -0.71 (t -1.7) | +0.31 (t +0.4); aligned breakout 1/2 -0.91 (t -2.6) | +0.56 (t +1.1); no cell passes (sign flips or negative). Alignment vs coin-flip direction on the same bars adds about +0.2 to +1.0 pt per trade gross (e.g. 1/2 exit: -0.71 vs -1.33 train, +0.31 vs -0.72 test) = a real but small directional edge, smaller than the 1-pt cost. Share of days with net >= +10 pts 13-47% but day sums negative.
228: 1m flips: gross -0.8 to +0.9 pts per trade, net -0.1 to -1.8, every cell negative net (aligned + vector exit: gross +0.27 | +0.93, net -0.73 | -0.07). 5m flips: aligned with the 15m vector and held to the opposite 5m flip: gross +2.51 | +2.83, net +1.51 | +1.83, t +0.6 | +0.4 (n 654 | 631), not significant; ATR exits negative. Higher-timeframe alignment lifts gross by about 1 pt per trade on both timeframes; nothing clears cost with significance.
Read: the vector's directional edge is worth roughly 0.5-1 pt per trade at scalp horizons; a 10-pt target leaves no room after a 1-pt cost. The legs that work carry 4-35 pts per trade.

## Report 229: fill models for payout droughts (xs/report_229_fill_models.py, log xs/report_229.log)
Rey Nov 17: a separate small model to fill the main algo's drought days; suggested 1:1 vector (stop = vector level, target = equal distance). NQ 2018-2026-07, cost 1 pt, train 2018-21 | test 2022-26, day-clustered t. Drought day = main algo trailing 40-day P&L <= 0 (shifted 1 day).
M_A 1:1 vector (stop = |entry - vector level|, R>=3 pts, target k x R):
- 5m flips: net negative at every k (-1.5 to -3.4 pts/trade).
- 15m flips: -1.4/-1.8 (0.5R) to +0.9/-1.0 (2R), not significant.
- 1H flips (n 215 | 256, median R 63 | 124 pts): 1R net +6.4 (t +1.6, win 53%) | +22.4 (t +3.3, win 57%); 2R +5.3 | +24.3 (t +3.4). ~0.1 trades/day. Per day (1R) +1.3 | +4.4 pts. Drought days: -0.4 | +6.2 (other days +1.8 | +4.2). Corr with main +0.21 | +0.26.
M_B VWAP fade 2.0/2.5 sigma: net -3.1 to -5.1 per trade (t -3.1 / -2.3), drought-day losses -19 to -26 pts/day in test, fails.
Read: the only candidate is 1H 1:1 vector; train not significant (t 1.3-1.7), test t ~3 on 14 cells (Bonferroni 3.0 marginal), low frequency, R of 60-120 pts is a big stop for a 50K (sizing 1 MNQ). A lead, not a fill model. Lucid-sim integration not yet run.

## Reports 230-231: fill models on the revised split + the 1H 1:1 vector inside the Lucid sim (xs/report_230_fill_models_gex.py, vx_trades.py, report_231_vx_in_lucid.py; logs xs/report_230.log, report_231.log)
Rey Nov 17: split changed to train 2019-21, test = 2018 + 2022-26; add dealer-gamma regime (prior-close QQQ net GEX, neg = <0 on 64% of days, low3 = bottom third of 252d rank, pos = complement); VWAP model is sigma bands (std of close-VWAP), target VWAP, stop same distance; if flips+gamma fail, test the 1H 1:1 in the sim.
230 (net pts/trade, cost 1, day-clustered t; train | test): 5m 1R all -1.9 (t-1.7) | -3.2 (t-2.8), neg -2.4 | -2.6, pos -1.1 | -4.2. 15m 1R all -0.9 | -1.4, neg -1.3 | -1.1, low3 +2.1 | -1.4 (nothing). 1H 1R all +5.2 (t+1.0, n166) | +20.4 (t+3.5, n304); neg +6.8 (t+1.0, n109) | +25.7 (t+3.6, n209); low3 +11.7 | +35.2 (n62|92); pos +2.1 | +8.8 (t+0.9). Same ordering (neg > pos) in both eras; 2R: neg +5.9 | +32.5 (t+4.3). VWAP fade 2.0/2.5 sigma: all -2.5/-2.0 | -4.2/-5.4 (t -2.1 | -2.8), pos gamma -2.4 | -0.2, neg gamma -2.6 | -6.3 (t-3.2): fails everywhere, only the neg-gamma direction is as predicted (fade worse there).
VX per year (1H 1:1, all, net pts/trade): 2018 +10, 2019 -2, 2020 +8, 2021 +9, 2022 +40, 2023 +5, 2024 +8, 2025 +35, 2026 +31; ~55 trades/yr.
231 Lucid 50K one account at a time 2018-26, 1H 1:1 leg at 1/2/3 MNQ (all days or neg-gamma days) vs main only. Default payout rule: main 0.53 payouts/month, longest gap 305 d; with VX 0.49-0.58 per month, longest gap 281-419 d. Full-payout rule: main 0.58/month, longest gap 197 d, $12.4k a year; with VX 0.45-0.55/month, longest gap 198-399 d, $9.5-11.7k a year. 2022-25 pass rate 82% -> 85-89% with VX at 1-2 MNQ all days; payout counts and droughts unchanged or worse.
Read: the 1H 1:1 vector is a real-looking edge in points (8 of 9 years positive, neg gamma helps) but it does not pad payouts: droughts are long stretches (2019, 2021 H2, 2024) where the main account is stuck or resetting, VX is thin (1 trade per ~5 days) and big-stop (R 60-120 pts), so one loss wipes the extra. Not adopted. Medians are fine (gap median 21-29 d); the tail (6-10 month gaps) is the problem and nothing tested here moves it.

## Report 232: exits for the 1H vector flip trades (xs/report_232_vx_exits.py, log xs/report_232.log)
Rey Nov 17: winners that come back to the stop cost twice (he believed the trailing drawdown counts open profit); test break-even and trails instead of 1:1. Lucid help page (MLL / EOD drawdown): the Max Loss Limit is recalculated after the session close from the highest closing balance; intraday open profit does not raise it. So a winner that returns to the stop costs only the missed profit, not extra drawdown. (The floor itself is still hit intraday by open losses; the sim's mae term models that.)
Same entries as 229-231; exits FIX 1R/2R, BE@.5R (tgt 1R), BE@1R (no tgt), vector-level trail, ATR(1H,14) trail x2/x3, 5m EMA20 cross, anchored-VWAP cross; cost 1 pt; train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
All days net pts (t): FIX1R +5.2 (1.0) | +20.5 (3.5); FIX2R +2.7 | +22.6; BE@.5R +5.8 (1.3) | +20.0 (3.6), avg loss -43 -> -28 | -65 -> -48; BE@1R +2.4 | +23.5 (3.8); vector trail +1.4 | +22.6; ATR2 +2.1 | +19.8; ATR3 -1.3 | +21.2; EMA20 +2.4 | +20.7; VWAP +3.3 (0.8) | +21.0 (3.7). Neg gamma: BE@.5R +8.8 (1.5) | +25.4; BE@1R +5.4 | +33.9 (4.3); vector trail +3.9 | +33.7 (4.3); VWAP +7.3 (1.2) | +27.4.
Open-profit giveback (MFE - exit) stays 38-69 pts in every exit; none keeps more of the move. Trades with MFE >= 1R that finish <= 0: 15-24% for BE@1R / vector trail in train, 5-15% in test; 0% for the 1R-target variants. Read: exits change the shape (smaller losses with BE@.5R, bigger winners with trails) but not the edge; entry carries it.

## Report 233: candle patterns paired with vector flips (xs/report_233_candles_vector.py, log xs/report_233.log)
Rey Nov 17: candle patterns before the flip, paired with the vector, instead of a separate model. 7 declared patterns (CLOSE strong close, BODY impulse, ENGULF, COMP compression, REJ rejection wick on bar before, PB3 three-bar pullback, GAPLVL decisive cross of old level) on 15m and 1H flips; exits FIX 1R and BE@.5R tgt 1R; train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26; TRUE vs FALSE net pts, Welch t of the difference.
1H FIX 1R (TRUE vs FALSE, train | test): BODY +7.0 vs -3.3 (t+0.9) | +23.4 vs +5.5 (t+1.6); GAPLVL +6.7 vs -3.6 (t+1.0) | +23.0 vs +6.7 (t+1.2); ENGULF +22.8 vs +2.3 (t+1.2, n23) | +37.0 vs +17.9 (t+0.9, n41); CLOSE, REJ ~0; COMP and PB3 n <= 12 in one era. 15m: every pattern flips sign or is ~0 between eras (REJ +4.0 train, -10.6 test; CLOSE -1.6 | -3.6 with FALSE +5.5 in test, t -1.9).
Read: no pattern passes (same sign both eras AND t >= 2 in test). On 1H, BODY, GAPLVL and ENGULF lean the same way in both eras (TRUE better, 'decisive displacement' theme) but t is 0.9-1.6 and they overlap; they only trim ~15% of trades. 15m flips stay edge-less with or without patterns.

## Report 234: 1H vector, break-even at +0.5R with 1R target, as a leg in the Lucid sim (xs/report_234_vxbe_in_lucid.py, vx_trades.vx(be=0.5), log xs/report_234.log)
Default payout rule (>= $500), one account 2018-26, main only 0.53 payouts/month, $9.0k/yr, longest gap 305 d, 2022-25 pass 82%: + VX BE all days 1 MNQ 0.62/month, $11.1k/yr, gap 307 d, pass 86%, start-to-first-payout (2018-21 starts) 85 -> 77 d; 2 MNQ 0.56, $10.0k, gap 281; neg-gamma only 0.51-0.56, $8.8-9.9k. Full-$2,000 payout rule: main 0.58/month, $12.4k/yr, gap 197 d; + VX BE 0.51-0.55/month, $10.8-11.7k, gap 204-248 d (worse). Read: BE exits help under the default payout rule (+0.09 payouts/month, +$2k/yr, pass rate +4 pts) but do not shorten the longest drought and lose to the full-payout rule alone.

## Report 235: state-conditioned models (xs/report_235_state_models.py, log xs/report_235.log)
Rey Nov 17: fair-value mean reversion; model the market by state (what is happening) and judge each state alone. State known before the trade: gamma (prior-close QQQ net GEX sign) x chop/trend (Kaufman ER of prior 10 daily closes, train cuts .22/.43). A: fade the first 5m close after 10:00 with |close - FV| >= K x 14d mean range, FV = prior-day RTH VWAP (fv1) or prior-5-day VWAP (fv5), target FV, stop same distance, cost 1. B: 15:30 -> 15:55 momentum with the day-so-far direction, stop 40. Train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
A, all days: fv1 K.5 -3.2 | -2.9, K.75 +0.6 | -4.7; fv5 K.5 +0.8 | -3.3, K.75 +3.3 | -5.8 (no edge). Positive gamma (mechanism prediction: fade works) is negative in BOTH eras (fv5 K.5 -3.7 | -5.7, K.75 -5.1 | -8.8): prediction failed in direction. Negative gamma: fv5 K.75 chop +8.8 (t1.1, n139) | +13.6 (t1.7, n293), trend +23.6 (1.8) | +9.9 (0.8); fv5 K.5 chop -0.8 | +16.5 (t2.4), trend +18.5 (1.6) | +13.5 (1.2); fv1 K.75 trend +18.4 (1.3) | +29.2 (2.0). But the neg-gamma all-ER cells fail in test (fv5 K.75 +9.7 | -4.2), so 'mid' ER days lose and the pattern is not coherent. Best fill candidate: fv5 K.75 neg-gamma chop: drought days +4.3 | +4.0 pts/day vs other +0.4 | +2.3, corr with main -0.06; about 48 cells read, so t 1-2.4 does not clear a ~3 bar.
B: late-day momentum all days +0.2 | -0.6; positive gamma -0.5 | -4.9 (t -3.2: momentum fails, reversal in test only), negative gamma +0.7 | +1.6, neg+trend +2.0 | +4.8 (t1.5). Not an edge.
Read: no state-conditioned model passes. A lead worth one more look: reversion to the 5-day VWAP when dealers are net short gamma (opposite of the textbook story: overshoots in short gamma snap back to multi-day value), low corr with the main algo, positive on drought days in both eras.

## Report 236: robustness of the 235 lead (reversion to multi-day VWAP in negative gamma) (xs/report_236_fv_negg_surface.py, log xs/report_236.log)
Surface FV window 3/5/10 d x K .5/.75/1.0, neg gamma, all ER: train positive in all 9 cells (+3.6 to +16.0, rising with K), test negative in all 9 (-1.0 to -10.3). ER split: chop and trend are positive but 'mid' ER loses -38 pts per trade in test (t -4.6, n 269; train -0.4 | +1.1), so the 235 pattern was the complement of one bad cell, not a state effect. Gamma as bottom-third rank: train +3.6/+7.8/+15.1, test -5.7/-6.8/-10.3. By year (FV5 K.75 neg): -5 +4 +8 +16 -12 +1 -23 +19 +9. Dead: no smooth surface, no stable state, no mechanism. Not a fill model.

## Report 237: order-flow proxies and options Greeks as filters on vector flips (xs/report_237_orderflow_options.py, log xs/report_237.log)
Rey Nov 17: orderflow and options data. 4 order-flow proxies from 5m bars (signed volume by close location; no tick data before Dec 2024) and 7 QQQ prior-close Greek features read in trade direction, on 1H and 15m flips, FIX 1R, TRUE vs FALSE net pts, train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
1H: ABSORB (6-bar cumulative delta before the flip is against it) +11.3 vs +1.6 (t+1.0, n61) | +36.9 vs +10.2 (t+2.1, n117): same sign both eras, best cell. IV high +14.3 vs -0.4 (t+1.3) | +29.6 vs +11.9 (t+1.5). DEX agree +11.8 vs +0.3 (t+1.1) | +6.7 vs +28.7 (t-2.0) and VANNA agree +9.5 vs -0.4 | +10.0 vs +29.0 (t-1.7): sign flips between eras. Strong delta (top third) +16.4 vs -2.4 (t+1.8) | +18.6 vs +22.1 (t-0.3): fails. DLT agree, RVOL, charm, room-to-wall, skew, put/call: ~0. 15m: every feature |t| < 1.5, ABSORB opposite sign (-5.2 vs +1.5 | -3.2 vs -0.4). 
Read: nothing passes (22 cells, bar ~3). The two same-sign leans on 1H (absorption before the flip, high IV) are small-n leads; DEX/vanna reverse between eras. Real tick CVD only exists Dec 2024-Nov 2025, too short to test.

## Report 238: absorption x negative gamma x high IV on vector flips, then lower timeframes (xs/report_238_absorb_negg_combo.py, log xs/report_238.log)
Rey Nov 17: if order flow works with the vector, order flow into negative gamma with the vector should work; if it filters too much, go lower. 1H flips FIX 1R, net pts per trade (t), train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26, trades/yr:
ALL +5.2 (1.0), 55/yr | +20.5 (3.5), 47/yr. ABSORB +11.3 (1.5) | +36.9 (3.4), ~19/yr. IVHI +14.3 (1.4) | +29.6 (3.7). NEG +6.8 (1.0) | +25.7 (3.6). ABSORB&NEG +19.1 (1.8) | +44.7 (3.0), 12/yr. ABSORB&IVHI +20.3 (1.4, n19) | +49.9 (2.9, n52). NEG&IVHI +11.9 | +26.1 (3.3). ABSORB&NEG&IVHI +29.1 (1.8, n16) | +44.2 (2.5, n45), 6/yr. Net per trade rises with each condition in both eras; train t stays <= 1.8 and frequency falls to 6-13 trades a year.
Lower timeframes (ABSORB, NEG, ABSORB&NEG): 30m ALL +0.9 | +1.5, ABSORB&NEG +4.8 | -6.2; 15m ALL -0.9 | -1.4, ABSORB&NEG -9.5 | -3.4; 5m ALL -1.9 | -3.2, ABSORB&NEG -4.1 | -3.3. No edge at 30m, 15m or 5m, with or without the filters.
Read: the 1H flip edge is a large-move edge (R 60-120 pts); conditioning sharpens it but cuts it to about one trade a month, and it does not transfer to lower timeframes. Selective overlay, not a filler.

## Report 239: noise-band trapped breakout (REJ) and absorbed-then-break continuation (CONT) with gamma, flow and IV state (xs/report_239_noise_trap.py, log xs/report_239.log)
Rey Nov 17: use absorption + gamma + IV on a different model for other days, a unique idea, with the noise range. Noise band as in orb_noise_vector (sigma(t) = 14-session mean |close/open-1| at the slot; UB = max(open, prior close)(1+sigma), LB mirrored). 5m, entries after 10:00, FIX 1R, cost 1 pt. REJ = bar pokes through a band and closes back inside, fade it (stop = poke extreme, R median 14-22 pts); CONT = first close beyond a band, go with it (stop = band, R 11-17 pts). Train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
REJ all -2.0 (t-1.7) | -1.6 (-1.2); pos gamma +0.6 | -5.1 (t-2.4); neg gamma -3.6 (-2.1) | -0.1; TRAP -2.8 | -1.1; pos&TRAP 0.0 | -4.8; IVHI -2.4 | -0.5; pos&TRAP&IVHI n30 -2.0 | -3.5. CONT all 0.0 | -1.0; neg gamma +0.8 | -1.1; ABS -5.9 (t-2.4) | -5.4 (-1.7); neg&ABS -8.4 (-2.6) | -6.0 (-1.5); neg&ABS&IVHI -9.6 | -3.1. Corr with main algo +0.2 for CONT (M1-like), ~0 for REJ.
Read: no edge; with 11-24 pt stops, a 1 pt cost and coin-flip win rates there is nothing to filter. Absorption before a noise-band break is NEGATIVE in both eras (breaks that follow opposite flow fail), the reverse of its effect on 1H flips. Consistent with every test so far: the edge exists only where stops are 60-120 pts.

## Reports 240-241: leverage on the rare 1H flips, and prior-day-range sweep fade (xs/report_240_overlay_in_lucid.py, report_241_pd_range_sweep.py; logs xs/report_240.log, report_241.log)
Rey Nov 17: try the failed-breakout idea, and put leverage on the rare high-percentage trades.
241: 1H bar sweeps the prior RTH day's high/low and closes back inside (1276 signals, median R 60 pts), fade, stop = sweep extreme, 1 pt cost. 1R: all -1.3 | -4.8 (t -1.4); TRAP -> +0.8 | -6.6; pos gamma -1.1 | +0.6; neg gamma -1.4 | -7.8; TRAP&pos +1.3 | -1.6; IVHI -0.4 | -7.8. 2R: all +0.8 | -7.7 (t -2.1); TRAP -0.2 | -9.9 (t -2.4); neg gamma +0.1 | -11.2 (t -2.3). No edge, and the test era loses (the sweeps tend to go on, but that is an observation after the fact, not tested).
240 (Lucid 50K, 1H ABSORB&NEG leg, 112 trades, 2018-26; ABSORB&NEG&IVHI 68 trades; FIX 1R; the filters were picked looking at the full sample): default payout rule, main only 0.53 payouts/month, $9.0k/yr, gap 305 d: ABSORB&NEG 2 MNQ 0.53, $9.3k, 299 d; 3 MNQ 0.54, $9.8k, 330 d; 5 MNQ 0.51, $8.8k, 335 d; 8 MNQ 0.35, $5.8k, 384 d (2022-25 pass 79%, paid once 49%). Triple 3/5/8 MNQ 0.46/0.44/0.41 per month, $7.7/7.2/6.6k. Full-$2,000 rule, main 0.58, $12.4k, gap 197 d: ABSORB&NEG 2 MNQ 0.58, $12.4k, gap 369 d; 3 MNQ 0.50, $10.6k; 5 MNQ 0.52, $11.1k, gap 240; 8 MNQ 0.45, $9.5k, gap 466 d; triple 3 MNQ 0.59, $12.7k, gap 385 d; 5 MNQ 0.52; 8 MNQ 0.51.
Read: leverage on the high-conviction 1H flips does not raise payouts/month or shorten droughts; at 8 MNQ a 60-120 pt stop is $960-1,920 on a $2,000 floor and payouts fall. Pass rate 2022-25 only moves 82 -> 83-84%.

## Report 242: pullbacks to VP / fib levels inside the 1H vector regime, plus a walk-forward regression (xs/report_242_level_pullbacks.py, log xs/report_242.log)
Rey Nov 17: mean reversion with fib or VP levels + vector context + regime classifier or regression. Entry: 5m bar after 10:00 trades to a prior-day level (POC, VAH, VAL, fib 38.2/50/61.8, volume profile 2-pt bins, 70% value area) between the 1H vector level and price and closes back on the vector side; stop = 1H vector level (R >= 15), target 1R/2R, cost 1 pt; 4,656 touches. Train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
Pooled first touch per day/side: 1R -3.9 (t-1.1, n485) | +5.7 (t+1.5, n934); 2R -4.3 | +8.3 (t+2.0). By level 2R: POC +1.3 | +11.9 (2.3), VAH +2.3 | +9.3, VAL -9.4 | +5.6, F382 -4.1 | +8.7, F50 -1.2 | +13.0 (2.2), F618 +2.4 (0.5) | +16.0 (2.9). ABS, NEG, IVHI filters do not add (pooled&ABS+NEG 2R -1.5 | +9.2). Opposite signs between eras for the pooled cells: fails the rule; only F618 and POC keep one sign, train t < 0.6.
Regression (L2 logistic, train 2019-21, standardised features: gamma, IV, absorption, ER, distance to vector, hours since open, level dummies; label = made money after cost): AUC 0.586 train | 0.527 test; coefficients small (largest VAH +0.17, ABS +0.12). Test top half +5.2 (t1.1) vs bottom half +2.4 (t0.5): no out-of-sample skill beyond the base rate.
By year pooled 2R: 2018 +3, 2019 -2, 2020 -2, 2021 -9, 2022 +13, 2023 -7, 2024 0, 2025 +25, 2026 +23. It pays in 2022, 2025, 2026 only, the same years the 1H flips pay (+40, +35, +31), and loses in 2019, 2021, 2023, the main algo's drought years. Same regime, not an independent source; not a filler.

## Report 243: which level is worth trading today - confluence ranking of candidate levels (xs/report_243_level_selection.py, log xs/report_243.log)
Rey Nov 17: use options flow + fib + order flow to pick which level to trade each day (POC, VAL, ledge, HVN, LVN). Candidate levels (12,586 touches): VP (POC, VAH, VAL, 3 HVN, 3 LVN), FIB (38.2/50/61.8 of prior range), EXT (prior-day H/L, overnight H/L), OPT (call/put wall); confluence = other families within 0.1 ATR14 (0-3); pullback to the level in the 1H vector regime as report 242. Train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
Confluence buckets (2R): conf 0 -5.8 | +6.7; conf 1 -0.9 | +4.2; conf 2+ +3.1 | +2.6; conf 2+&ABS -1.3 | +5.0. By type (2R): HVN -5.5 | +11.4 (t2.4), LVN +1.0 | +8.3, PDH +1.7 | +6.1, PDL -11.4 | +10.5, ONH -0.8 | +4.8, ONL -6.0 | +7.3, CW +0.3 | +5.5, PW -6.6 | +4.4, POC +1.3 | +11.9 (2.3), VAH +2.3 | +9.3, VAL -9.4 | +5.6; families VP -1.8 | +5.5, FIB -2.6 | +11.0 (2.2), EXT -2.6 | +6.2, OPT -6.9 | +4.5. Test era positive almost everywhere, train flat/negative: the 242 trend-year effect again; confluence does not separate in train.
TOP-1 per day/side by (confluence + ABS): 1R +4.2 (t1.5) | +13.1 (t4.5); 2R +4.0 | +15.4 (t4.9). HINDSIGHT: it picks the best score of the whole day, 37% of its trades are not the first touch (a better touch comes later). Causal version (first touch of the day/side that reaches the score): score>=1 -3.8 | +1.7, >=2 -0.5 | +0.5, >=3 +0.9 | +0.9, conf>=2 +1.1 | +0.4 (1R); 2R +0.4 to +4.6 in test, -3.8 to +2.1 in train, all t < 1.4. Nothing survives.
Read: confluence ranking does not choose a better level in real time; the TOP-1 headline was a selection artifact of knowing the day's later touches. The era effect (test positive, train flat) is the same trending-year regime as the vector flips.

## Report 244: three-stage level reaction as a side model for the other days (xs/report_244_three_stage_level.py, log xs/report_244.log)
Rey Nov 17: not main alpha; stage 1 level with a reason (fib / call-put walls, lazy version allowed), stage 2 rejection, stage 3 absorption; gate if needed. Not tied to the vector. Stage 2 = 5m bar touches the level from the near side, closes back with a >= 50% wick; fade next open, stop = bar extreme + 1 (R >= 10), 1R/2R. Stage 3 = delta of the 3 bars into the level in the push direction AND touch-bar volume >= 1.3 x prior-12 mean. 7,597 rejections, 1,137 absorbed. Train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26.
Funnel (fib + wall levels, 2R, net pts): rejection only -0.6 | -1.4 (t -1.1); + delta -0.3 | -0.2; + volume +0.1 | +2.3; + both +2.2 (t0.5, n74) | +6.7 (t1.6, n193). Fib only +1.7 | +8.0 (1.8); walls only +5.5 | +8.7 (n22 | 56); fib&wall confluence +1.2 | +8.3 (t2.2). 1R: +0.4 | +1.6. Menu-wide conf >= 2 (2R): rejection only -1.6 | -2.2, + absorption +0.2 (n33) | +6.4 (t1.7, n145); chop gate (ER bottom third) +3.0 | +18.0 (t2.7, n11 | 46); positive-gamma gate -5.2 | +3.3; IVHI +3.0 | -2.7. Placebo (absorbed rejection at conf 0 levels, 2R) -3.5 | +2.8.
By year (fib/wall stage 3, 2R): 2018 +6, 2019 +7, 2020 -2, 2021 +4, 2022 +9, 2023 +9, 2024 +16, 2025 -3, 2026 -2 (n 9-50 per year). Menu-wide conf >= 2: +9 +15 +11 -13 +3 +2 +10 +4 +22. Positive in the drought years 2019, 2021 (fib/wall version) and 2024, unlike the vector-year pattern of 242-243.
Read: each stage improves the result in both eras (the staged idea behaves), same sign both eras at 2R, but t 0.5-2.2, ~25 trades a year, average +2 to +7 pts per trade after cost. A lead with the right year profile, underpowered; the 11-24 pt stops mean cost eats the 1R version.

## Report 245: every level source + OPTICS clustering, three-stage reaction (xs/report_245_all_levels_optics.py, log xs/report_245.log)
Rey Nov 17: use everything (VP on several time frames, extremes, swing points/ledges, shelves, fib, walls) and an OPTICS clustering of price acceptance instead of VP peak-picking; more trades; drop VP if it does not work. 82 levels/day (CLU 55.8k, SWG 37.6k, VPd 17.2k, VP3 16.9k, VPw 15.1k, EXT 12.8k, FIB 12.3k, VPm 8.9k, OPT 3.2k level-days); 27,359 rejection rows, 11,805 unique rejections, 1,239 absorbed (stage 3). Same stages as 244. Train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26, net pts per trade, trades/yr.
Stage 3 pooled over ALL groups: 1R -1.4 (t-1.1, 104/yr) | -1.0 (139/yr); 2R -1.1 | +0.2: trades x4-5 vs 244's ~25/yr, edge gone; by year ~0 every year (2R -2..+3). Stage 2 alone, all groups: -1.6 | -1.7 (t -3.7 | -5.4) at 1R: the rejection by itself loses.
By group (2R): VPd -2.0 | +3.1, VP3 +2.3 | +1.2, VPw -0.1 | +5.1, VPm -5.8 | +5.9, EXT +4.1 | -0.4, SWG (ledges) +1.7 | +1.3 (71/yr), FIB +3.1 | +4.8 (t1.4), OPT walls +6.9 | +13.7 (t2.2, n 23 | 59, ~9/yr), CLU (OPTICS) -1.1 | +2.4 (88/yr, t 1.0). VP groups only: -2.9 | +0.5; CLU only: -1.1 | +2.4; without any VP -0.5 | +1.4; EXT+SWG+FIB+OPT +1.9 | +0.9. Confluence by number of groups (2R): 1 -7.9 (t-2.9) | -2.9, 2 +3.9 | -4.5, 3+ -0.1 | +4.4 (t1.7): not monotonic.
Read: VP levels do not carry the reaction (drop them as Rey said), OPTICS clusters are no better than VP peaks, swing points add trades with ~0 edge. Adding level sources multiplies trade count but dilutes the edge to zero; what survives is the small fib + call/put-wall set from 244 (~9-36 trades a year). Trade count and edge trade off one-for-one here.

## Reports 246-247: context + order-flow model over all level groups, and explicit level 'stories' (xs/report_246_context_orderflow_model.py, report_247_level_stories.py; logs xs/report_246.log, report_247.log)
Rey Nov 17: levels are context, the story is the confluence (call wall at the 1-day HVN, fib at the 5-day POC), pivots as liquidity, poor highs/lows, options flow, volatility, order flow; ignore levels that do not matter. 246: 10,250 stage-2 rejections over 10 level groups (VPd/VP3/VPw/VPm, EXT, SWG pivots, EQ equal highs/lows, POOR poor highs/lows, FIB, OPT walls; 63 levels/day) with presence flags, group count, delta into the level, volume ratio, absorption flag, gamma, IV, chop ER, vector alignment, hour, R/ATR; L1 logistic (kept 7 features, tiny weights: FIB +.03, delta +.05, vector +.04), 2R outcome. Train 2019-21 -> test 2018+2022-26: AUC 0.543 | 0.504; all candidates -1.9 (t -2.8) | -2.3 (t -4.6); rule stage 3 +0.7 | -0.4; model top 20% +1.1 (in-sample) | -1.6 (t -1.5); top 10% +1.2 | -1.2. Expanding walk-forward top 20%: -1.7 (t -1.4), by year 2018 -1, 2022 -4, 2023 -3, 2024 +1, 2025 -1, 2026 -1.
247: all 45 pairs of level groups x {all, absorbed}, n >= 25 in both eras (50 cells): positive in both eras 11, negative in both 23, mixed 16. Best (absorbed, 2R, train | test): VPd+VPw +1.8 | +10.0 (t1.9), VPw+FIB +3.8 | +9.4, SWG+FIB +2.6 | +7.0, EXT+EQ +3.9 | +6.0, VP3+VPw +5.8 | +5.7, EXT+SWG +6.6 | +3.1; all train t <= 1.4, test t <= 1.9. L1 with 45 pair products + pair x absorption: kept 10 of 111, AUC 0.548 | 0.501; test top 10% -1.3, walk-forward top 10% -0.8 (t -0.4), by year 0, -1, -2, +1, -2, -2.
Read: stacking the context features does not predict which rejections work; the model has no out-of-sample skill (AUC 0.50). The stacked multi-timeframe VP / fib / swing pairs with absorption lean positive (11 of 50, test-heavy), but at t <= 1.9 and with the test era positive almost everywhere, they are the same era effect as 242-243, not a story the data separates. The only survivor remains the fib + call/put wall + absorption set from 244 (~10-30 trades a year).

Note on VP3+VPw (247): it works in both eras with the same size (absorbed, 2R: +5.8 (t1.1, n49) | +5.7 (t1.2, n142), ~21 trades/yr); it is not significant, not negative.

Vector WITH vs AGAINST the trade on 5m/15m/30m/1H/4H/1D: all rejections WITH -0.7..-2.0 | -1.7..-2.4, AGAINST -1.5..-2.9 | -1.5..-2.3 (all negative); absorbed WITH -1.2..+2.6 | -0.5..+1.7, AGAINST -0.4..+4.4 | -3.0..+0.2: sign flips between eras (1D AGAINST&absorbed +4.4 | -3.0, 4H +0.8 | -2.9). Alignment count of 6 TFs: 4-6 agree -0.8 | -2.5 (absorbed -0.5 | -0.9), 0-1 agree absorbed +3.5 | -9.4 (t -2.8): no pattern. Options flow (dealer delta, vanna flow, charm, put/call, skew, IV, net gamma) in the trade direction, all and absorbed: every cell |t| < 1.4 or sign-flipping (vanna & absorbed +4.4 | -1.0, charm & absorbed +2.7 | -2.9, IV high & absorbed 0.0 | -4.1, not IV high & absorbed +1.2 | +2.7).
249 (Lucid 50K, extra leg: absorbed rejections at fib / call-put wall levels, 381 trades, 44/yr, +5.5 pts net (train +3.7 | test +6.1), median R 24; or that plus the 10 positive pairs, 708 trades, 82/yr, +1.9 net; dollar-risk sizing $150 or $300 per trade): default payout rule, main 0.53 payouts/month, $9.0k/yr, gap 305 d, 2022-25 pass 82%: fib/wall $150 0.53, $8.9k, 298 d, pass 87%; fib/wall+pairs $150 0.51, $8.4k, 417 d, pass 89%; fib/wall $300 0.62, $11.1k, 321 d, pass 87%, paid once 67%; fib/wall+pairs $300 0.52, $9.1k, 289 d. Full-$2,000 rule, main 0.58, $12.4k, gap 197 d: fib/wall $150 0.49, $10.4k, 260 d; +pairs $150 0.47, $9.9k, 399 d; fib/wall $300 0.50, $10.6k, 463 d; +pairs $300 0.48, $10.1k, 474 d.
Read: vector direction on any time frame and the other options-flow techniques do not select the rejections that work. The level legs lift the 2022-25 pass rate 82 -> 85-89% and, only under the default payout rule at $300 risk, payouts to 0.62/month and net to $11.1k a year; under the full-payout rule every version is worse than main alone and the longest gap grows. Not a drought filler.

## Reports 252-253: mean-reversion ceiling + causal day-type predictors (VIX, gamma, gap)
252 (declared): generic VWAP fade (k ATR from VWAP, stop 0.5-1 ATR beyond, target VWAP, cost 1pt) split by EX-POST day type (RTH efficiency tertiles). k=.75/stop 1.0: MR days train +28.9 (n19) / test +109 (n25, win 88%); TREND days -41 / -81; all days -9. So the ceiling is huge, the payoff is not the problem. Main algo pts/day by ER tercile: MR -1.8 / -0.6, MID +2.0 / +2.9, TREND +10.7 / +18.0 (train/test).
253 (declared directions): day-level AUC for MR vs TREND of prior-close VIX falling / VIX high / small gap / QQQ net GEX>0 / low IV / prior-day ER low = 0.47-0.55 (gexpos .53/.55 best, weak). Fade P&L ON vs OFF not consistent across splits and across k; only vixfall&gexpos looked good at k=.5 (+5/+14.5) and failed at k=.75 test (-11.8): treat as noise. Main algo daily pts correlation with VIX change 0.03, with prior VIX level 0.01, with day ER 0.23. Conclusion: day type is not predictable from daily context; VIX is uncorrelated with the algo so it cannot gate or hedge it. Next: intraday causal state (VWAP crossings, early ER, realized vs IV expected move).

## Report 254: intraday causal state -> does the rest of the day mean-revert (checkpoints 10:30, 11:00; label = efficiency of the REST of the day)
AUC (MR_after vs TREND_after, train 2019-21 | test 2018+2022-26), hypothesis direction fixed before: at 11:00 low ATRP rank .53|.53, call/put volume high .55|.54, QQQ net GEX>0 .55|.55 count; absorption (delta against the move) counts at 10:30 only (.54|.52). NOT working: vector flip count (.51|.54 weak), volume share at extremes (.55|.49), overnight inventory (.50|.48), range-used vs IV (.49|.53). Taper had the OPPOSITE sign to the declared hypothesis in both splits (.46|.46: MORE volume on the later extreme pushes -> MR); post-hoc, needs a declared retest. All effects small (AUC <= .57).

## Report 255: trade the extreme (rejection at session extreme >= .5 ATR from open; volume building, absorption, CVD divergence)
V0 rejection only: 229 trades/yr, -1.0 (train) | -1.4 (test) pts net at 1R, t -1.0/-1.6: loses. V1 +volume building (event-bar volume >= earlier extreme bars and >=1.3x prior-12): 24/yr, +3.3 | +2.0 at 1R (t .9/.5), 2R -1.6 | +6.6: the only filter that flips the sign, but t < 1.5 and 2R disagrees across splits. V2 +absorption: 42/yr, +2.2 | -0.6 at 1R. V3 +CVD divergence: ~94% of events qualify, adds nothing (-1.3 | -1.1). V5 V1+V2+V3: 17/yr, 1R +2.4 | +4.1, 2R -4.8 | +9.9, t <= 1.4. V4 by year (1R): 2020-24 positive, 2018/19/25 negative (2025 -11). Est-delta CVD vs real tick CVD (Dec 2024-Nov 2025): 92% agree on divergence, so the estimated delta is an adequate proxy. Verdict: not an edge yet; volume building at the later pushes is the only direction worth keeping.

## Report 256: event/news context (08:30 releases, NFP, opex, month-end, big-tech earnings reaction/night) vs day type, main algo and the extreme fade
No news/calendar feed on disk: flags derived (08:30 bar range >=2.5x 60d median = release; first Friday = NFP; third Friday = opex; last session = month-end; AAPL MSFT GOOGL AMZN META NVDA TSLA AVGO after-close reports -> reaction day next session, earnings night). MR-day share barely moves with any flag (25-42% vs ~34% base): events do not identify the day type. Main algo pts/day on event days (train | test): NFP +4.5 | +20.4 (vs +3.4 | +6.3); release +4.3 | +9.4 (vs 3.4 | 6.3); big-tech reaction day +3.1 | +19.4 (vs 3.5 | 5.6) = train flat, test large: era-dependent; reaction count dose-response in test (1 report +12.8, 2 +21.7, base +4.8; n 141/39). Report-255 fade (V0) on NFP or big-tech reaction days: 1R -4.5 | -5.7, 2R -8.3 | -8.7 (test t -2.9, same sign both splits) = do not fade on news days. Opex / month-end fade: 1R +3.2 | +3.0 (t .9 | 1.0, n 55 | 118), unstable by year. Not tested: FOMC/CPI dates (no calendar), consensus surprises, Sunday-night futures gaps.

## Report 257: (A) implied vol vs day type, (B) AMD/PO3/Wyckoff sweep-and-reclaim
A: QQQ prior-close IV rank, IV change, skew, prior realized/implied ratio vs today's RTH efficiency: Spearman |rho| <= .08 in both splits (IV does not flag choppy days); contemporaneous IV change vs ER +.04|+.04. Realized/implied persists for SIZE of range (ratio5 vs range/implied +.20|+.14) but not for efficiency. Mean range/implied by ER tercile MR 1.03, MID 1.20, TREND 1.43.
B: Asia range (20:00-02:00 ET) sweep then 5m close back inside, enter next open toward the opposite side, stop beyond the sweep, cost 1pt: 250 trades/yr, 1R +1.8|+0.9, 2R +3.9|+5.3 (t 1.4|1.9), opposite side +2.4|+0.7; win 41-42% at 2R, median R 48|68 pts. Stale-level placebo (Asia range 3 sessions old): ~62 trades/yr, 2R -0.1|+0.8: no effect. Prior-day-range sweep (Wyckoff spring/upthrust): 2R -0.2|+3.0 (n 405|687), not consistent. 'Tight Asia range' qualifier is true on ~99% of days (useless). Day-type split of the Asia 2R P&L: TREND days +31.8|+37.0, MID -3.1|+0.2, MR days -12.3|-17.6: the sweep-and-reclaim reversal is a trend-day pattern, NOT a mean-reversion filler. By year 2R pts (1 ct): 2018 -319, 2019 -50, 2020 +1818, 2021 +1132, 2022 +1417, 2023 -416, 2024 +2388, 2025 +1638, 2026 (Jan) +1888: negative in the same drought years as the main algo (2018/19/23). Daily corr with main algo +.11 (+.18 train, +.08 test). State tags: sweeps that hold are slightly more trend days (TREND 38-43% vs 30-34%) than reclaims, both splits.

## Report 259: FOMC decision days (LSE US.FOMC Minutes release dates minus 21 days; 63 sessions matched 2018-Jan 2026)
Declared direction (fade should LOSE on FOMC days) was WRONG, so this is post-hoc. Day type: MR share 25% | 36% (train|test) vs 34% | 33% other; not a clean MR day. Report-255 extreme fade on FOMC days: 55 trades / 39 days, 1R +12.8 (t 2.4, win 60%), 2R +20.8 (t 2.3, win 49%); train +11.1 | test +13.9 (1R), +13.8 | +25.5 (2R) vs about -2 on other days; positive in 6 of 8 years (2019 -4.6, 2024 -26.8 at 2R). Asia sweep leg LOSES on FOMC days (2R -6.8 | -6.3 vs +4.3 | +5.7). Main algo on FOMC days: +0.8 | +13.7 (train|test), by year -9.4 (2021) .. +33 (2022): era-dependent. Mechanism to test: the market waits for the 14:00 release (compression/chop before it), so entries before 14:00 should carry the edge; entry time not stored in ext255 (needs a rerun). ~7 trades/yr, so a small side leg at best, not a drought fix. Data: apply same for CPI/NFP when those exports arrive. Minutes parquet has no actual/consensus values (dates only).

## Report 260: FOMC fade by entry time + in the Lucid sim; literature scan (Nov 17)
Entry time (FOMC days, 55 trades / 39 days): before 14:00 n 23, 1R +9.9 (t 1.7), 2R +25.0 (t 2.4), train +47.2 (n8) | test +13.2 (n15); at/after 14:00 n 32, 1R +14.8, 2R +17.8 (t 1.3), train -5.3 (n14) | test +35.8 (n18). Both sides positive overall but neither side is consistent across splits: effective sample ~39 days.
Lucid sim ($300 risk, leg 'FM' in slot 10; paymin $500 | $2,000): baseline 24/20, 51 pays, $73.8k, first 84d, p90 gap 161, max 305, months paid 41%, worst12 $713 | 17/14, $100.8k, 112d, 136, 197, 44%, $1,800.
+ FOMC fade 2R pre-14:00 only: 24/21, 51 pays, $71.0k, first 63d, p90 104, max 299, months paid 46%, worst12 $1,694, nopay evals 5 vs 8 | 19/16, 55 pays, $99.0k, 82d, 128, 194, 46%, $1,800.
+ FOMC fade 1R all-day: $75.1k, p90 126, max 308 | $88.2k, max gap 392. + FOMC fade 2R all-day: $66.4k | $91.8k. Only the pre-14:00 variant helps timing without costing much (23 trades in 8 years: could be luck at the eval dates).
Literature (abstract-level reading only): Lucca-Moench (NY Fed sr512): realized vol and volume are LOWER before the FOMC release then jump at 2:15 (2:00 later); return from just before the release to the close ~0 -> pre-release compression mechanism. Double-well intraday diffusion (SSRN 3400384): no news -> mean reversion by noise traders, news -> momentum/overreaction; no trading results reported. 0DTE/dealer gamma blog (Resonanz, cites Adams SSRN 2026): long-gamma dealers are counter-trend, negative gamma amplifies; secondary source. Langevin potentials (arXiv 2309.12082): order-2 wells fit most windows, calm vs turbulent look similar, no backtest. Hawkes critical reflexivity (arXiv 1302.1405): E-mini branching ratio ~1 all years, kernel timescale shrinking. Quantum Schrodinger-like trading equation (arXiv 2401.05823): daily Chinese equities in-sample, no OOS, no backtest -> no usable edge. Propagator / transient impact (arXiv 2205.07385 review): price impact decays, so volume absorbed without price progress should revert (maps to volume-building lead).

## Reports 261-263: transient-impact absorption, Hawkes branching ratio, pre-release (8:30) compression
261 (propagator, declared: flow-justified displacement I=beta*Q reverts, coef negative and below the unexplained part U): 9,785 checkpoint obs 10:30-14:30, 60-min response. Coef on I: +0.022 (t .7) train | +0.059 (t 2.3) test, i.e. CONTINUATION not reversion; U +0.008 | +0.043. Fade top-decile |I| for 60 min: -0.7 | -2.5 pts/trade (n 435 | 632). Absorbed flow (big |Q|, tiny |U|): with flow +0.9 | +1.1 (t .3-.4), against -2.9 | -3.1. Verdict: no transient-impact reversion at the 60-min horizon on NQ 5m with est. delta; propagator decay is faster than this and the delta proxy is crude.
262 (Hawkes exp-kernel branching ratio n by 11:00 from 18:00 prior evening, events = |ret|/slot-median >= 2): median n 0.0 (fit collapses to Poisson on most days), AUC of -n for MR_after vs TREND_after .486 | .478, event count .523 | .499; erA by n tercile .483/.475/.469. No signal.
263 (pre-8:30 compression fade on NFP days, 04:00-08:25, flat before release): NFP 27 trades/17 days 1R -2.5, 2R -8.8 (t -1.4); other weekdays 457 trades 2R +2.0. NFP 08:30 bar range median .32 ATR vs .08 other; 52% of NFP days move >= .3 ATR in that bar vs 6%. No pre-release compression edge; FOMC does not generalize to NFP with this rule.

## Report 264: volatility zones + orderflow (forecast the day range, anchor the candidate extreme, target the rest of the range)
Range forecast (log RTH range, out-of-sample R2 train | test; MAE pts): HAR .507 | .330 (61 | 89); HAR + QQQ implied move .557 | .392 (60 | 86) = best, chosen on train; HAR + Yang-Zhang 5d .509 | .326 (no gain over HAR). IV adds real information about the SIZE of the day's range. Realized/forecast median ratio .90 | .95.
Standalone reversal at a rejected new session extreme (10:00-14:00), stop beyond the anchor, target = anchor +/- f x R^: all candidates 1R-ish loses: T=.618 R^ -3.1 | -3.2 (n 825 | 2,013, win 19%); T=1.0 R^ -2.4 | -2.7. By U (range already used) no bucket works. Orderflow: V1 volume building +/-: .618 -2.6 | +7.8 (n 42 | 83); V1&V2 .618 +0.8 | +23.2 (n 29 | 48, t 1.8 test) - too few trades, not significant in train.
Diagnostic: a rejected new extreme is the DAY extreme only 18% | 16% of the time (rest of day from the anchor: median .67 R^); with volume building 34% | 31% (n 47 | 89), V1&V2 30% | 31%: a consistent doubling in both splits. The bottleneck is identifying the extreme, not the target; the full-R^ target is too far (median remaining range .67 R^, higher .9 when U >= .5 or V1/V2). Not a leg yet.

## Reports 265-266: running (intraday-updating) volatility model, exhaustion at the extreme, running zones
265: R^_t (full-day range re-learned from range so far, realized vol, Rogers-Satchell vol and the 09:30 HAR+IV forecast) correlates .90 with the final range vs .65 for the 09:30 forecast. At checkpoints where price is within .15 of a fresh session extreme (1,559 obs; the extreme is the day's final one only 9% | 12%, train | test): AUC for HELD, train | test: share of expected range spent X .585 | .567 (best), push aggression fading .543 | .580, close location of the last 3 bars far from the extreme (.418 | .385 inverted = counts), volume dry-up counts weakly (.411 | .479), CVD divergence INVERTED (.410 | .390: weaker flow at a new extreme means it does NOT hold), effort/result .508 | .535, vanna z .504 | .520, gamma sign .487 | .505. Fade at the extreme (stop extreme+1) loses with every target: half range -5.0 | -5.0, VWAP -4.3 | -4.3, EMA20 -3.9 | -4.7 (win 31-32%), mid -5.2 | -5.3; the exhaustion score (>=2 of X, aggression fade, close location, volume dry-up) does not fix it (EMA20 -6.4 | -4.6). Held rate rises with X: .12 train / .19 test at X .9-1.0 (n 40 | 162).
266 (running zones Z_hi = low so far + R^_t, Z_lo = high so far - R^_t; zone touch + rejection, stop beyond the zone + .1 R^_t, median stop 122 pts): calibration: the final extreme lands within +/-.15 R^_t of the zone on 17-20% of days at 10:30, 44-48% at 13:30; the zones are systematically too far (median error -.32 R^_t for the high, +.35 for the low at 10:30; -.15/+.17 by 13:30). Zone fade vs placebo (new extreme outside the zone): zone 142 | 448 trades, VWAP -2.0 | -3.0, EMA20 +0.1 | -5.9, mid -1.7 | -2.0; placebo VWAP -5.9 | -7.1, EMA20 -7.1 | -8.0, mid -7.8 | -8.4 (t -3.7 test): the zone beats the placebo by 4-8 pts in BOTH splits, but still no net edge. By share of range spent: X .8-1.0 (n 100 | 308): mid +2.2 | +1.4, EMA20 +2.2 | -2.6, VWAP -1.0 | -0.3; X >= 1.0 (range already beyond the forecast; n 41 | 137): -4 to -13 in both splits = the extreme keeps going. Lead: X >= 1 looks like a trend-day / continuation state; X .8-1 is the only mildly mean-reverting zone.

## Report 267: range exceeded (session range so far >= thr x the running forecast R^_t): continuation test
My inference from 265-266 (fade loses when X >= 1 -> continuation wins) was WRONG. Event X >= 1.0 (n 223, 28/yr), enter in the push direction, stop .25 R^_t (median 60 pts): 1R -3.9 | -13.6 (t -2.2 test), 2R -6.7 | -13.3, to close -8.3 | -4.9; X >= 1.25 (n 38): train -26..-34 (t -3.5..-10), test +25/-12/+23 (unstable, n 12 | 26). Placebo X >= 0.8: 1R +0.8 | +1.7, to close -0.7 | +5.0 (t ~1). Rest-of-day efficiency after the event .40-.49 = normal (base ~.48), so it is NOT a trend state; it is a whipsaw/high-vol state where both the fade and the continuation hit their stops. By entry time: before 11:30 to-close +2.6 (n 72), after 11:30 -9.7 (n 151). Main algo pts/day on days flagged exceeded by 14:00: +7.0 vs +4.0 (train), +15.9 vs +6.2 (test): big-range days are the algo's days (flag overlaps the day's own P&L, so not tradable as a filter).

## Report 268 - quantile range forecasts (HAR vs YZ vs IV) + zone fade by day type and gamma
Declared before results. (A) Quantile regression q10/q50/q90 of log RTH range, rolling 500d: pinball x100 train|test: HAR 10.49|9.66, HAR+IV 10.02|9.28, HAR+YZ5 10.52|9.70, HAR+YZ20 10.53|9.72, HAR+IV+YZ20 10.09|9.34, IV+YZ20 (no HAR) 10.24|9.37. Coverage of [q10,q90] ~77-80% everywhere. YZ adds nothing over HAR; IV is the only gain. Rey's recollection (YZ better for zones) NOT reproduced; closest is IV+YZ20 without HAR being best calibrated (80%|79%).
(B) Zone fade X in [.8,1.0), pts to VWAP target, train|test: MR days (ex-post) +27|+33, MID +5|+14, TREND -20|-40 (hindsight only: it is the day-type label restated). Prior-close QQQ gamma (causal): positive +5|+5 (EMA +12|+2), negative -4|-3. Placebo ~-7|-9 both gammas. Positive gamma helps in sign in both splits (~8-10 pts) but t<1.7: lead, not edge. Not run in Lucid sim.

## Report 269 - GJR-GARCH, skew, random-walk (Bachelier/reflection) null for "extreme holds"
Declared before results. Window 300d (NQ data starts 2017-04, 500d dropped 2018). (A) Pinball x100 train|test: HAR 10.53|9.66, HAR+IV 9.81|9.24, +GJR 9.88|9.31, +skew 9.79|9.27, HAR+GJR 10.75|9.81. GJR hurts, skew neutral: IV stays the only useful addition. (B) Driftless random-walk null (P no new extreme = 2Phi(d/s_rem)-1, s_rem from HAR+IV range forecast, variance time profile from train): empirical hold rate tracks the null closely; excess +0..+5 pts mid-range both splits, near the extreme (null<.4) excess +3|+4 pts at X<.6, ~0 at X>=.8; gamma+ +2|+4 vs gamma- +1|+2. The random walk explains nearly all of it; volume-building (report 255, 34%|31% vs 17%) is a much larger effect than anything here.
## Report 270 - PCA
Declared before results. First-2h path PCA (PC1 86% = drift, PC2 7%, PC3 3%): predicts rest-of-day continuation and efficiency with train R2 <=.007 and test R2 <=0 (corr test -.05). Terciles show only a small positive continuation drift in the test split (t~2) with no train support. Context PCA (HAR, IV, skew, GEX, vanna, charm) -> day efficiency: raw R2 .020|-.002, PCA3 .005|-.002. Nothing there; day-type is not predictable from these inputs (consistent with 253/257).

## Report 271 - PCA + vector state; volume-building (VB) standalone trigger
Declared before results. (A) cont ~ PCA3 R2 .005|-.015; vector only (regime aligned w/ drift, flips, distance to level) .008|-.024; PCA+vector .012|-.030. Vector aligned with the first-2h drift: mean continuation -.003 (t-.1) train | +.046 (t+3.1) test: tiny, test-only, era effect (2022+ trend years), no predictive R2. Pairing does not help.
(B) VB at new extreme (>=2 earlier extreme bars, vol>=their mean & >=1.3x prior 12 bars, >=.5 R20 from open, no wick requirement), pts net, train|test: E0 (next open) 1R -3.3|+0.6, 2R -5.8|+1.2, VWAP -7.9|-0.4; E1 (confirmation break of event bar) 1R -1.0|+2.7, 2R +1.3|+0.9, VWAP -0.4|+3.4 (all |t|<=.6 except E0 train 2R/VWAP negative). Placebo without VB: -2..-4 (t to -2.4). VB beats placebo by ~3-5 pts but net ~0: holding the extreme more often (report 255) does not become a payoff with stop beyond the extreme and R 10-120. Vector cut uninformative: at a fresh extreme the vector regime is almost never opposite the push (n 1-4). Conclusion: no standalone mean-reversion trigger; stop searching for fade fillers. Next: main-algo drought diagnosis.

## Report 272 - drought diagnosis (windows vs rest, base-size daily $)
Declared before results. $/day | share of days >= $150 | <= -$150 | MR share | mean RTH range pts: W1 2019-03..2020-01 36 | .27 | .20 | .38 | 85; W2 2021-06..12 81 | .32 | .24 | .30 | 186; W3 2024-03..09 115 | .34 | .26 | .40 | 264; rest 117 | .31 | .22 | .29 | 236. Qualifying days a month are NOT scarce in the windows (mean 5.9 | 7.0 | 7.3 vs 6.7; months with <5: .18 | 0 | 0 vs .25). Algo $/day by type, window vs rest: TREND 178 | 253 | 310 vs 328; MID -5 | -17 | +114 vs +61; MR -25 | -17 | -17 vs -21. W1 = low-range era (85 pts a day; trend days pay about half in $ because sizes are fixed), W2 = trend days pay less and MID days negative, W3 = normal expectancy ($115/day) so a path/timing drought, not a performance one. Lever: expected-range (vol-targeted) sizing for W1-type windows; not tested in the sim yet.
## Report 273 - vector on other assets (ETF proxies, 15m RTH, flip-follow, 2 bps)
No GC/CL futures on disk. NQ-tuned vector parameters unchanged. train | test mean % per trade: QQQ +.037 (t.4) | +.067 (t1.2); SPY -.05 | +.02; IWM 0 | -.01; DIA -.07 | -.02; GLD +.05 | -.06; SLV -.08 | +.01; TLT +.03 | -.01; USO +.20 (t.9) | +.03; FXE -.051 (t-3.0) | -.046 (t-3.1); FXB -.035 | -.048 (t-2.8); FXY 0 | -.044. Raw flip-following does not transfer (not even to QQQ at |t|>2); currencies lose consistently (0 of 9 years positive FXE/FXB) but the fade gross is only ~+.026%/trade vs 4 bps cost: no edge. The NQ edge is in the intraday execution legs, not in the raw flip.

## Report 274 - stream view: block bootstrap of the daily P&L through a simplified Lucid Flex chain
Declared before results. Daily $ stream (shareable250, 2018-02..2026-01), simplified chain (no intraday MAE, no E2/F0 policies, daily loss clipped -$1,000, fee $90/eval + $100/mo, 1.45 calendar days per trading day), stationary block bootstrap mean 20 days, 1,000 paths x 2,087 days. Validation on history: $500 rule .77 payouts/mo, longest gap 9.0 mo (full sim ~300d, so gap matches; frequency is probably overstated vs the full sim's ~.5/mo); $2,000 rule gap 9.9 mo vs full sim 197d (does not match: policies matter). Bootstrap (all days) $500: .75/mo (p10 .66, p90 .84), longest gap median 7.0 mo (p90 9.9), months with >=1 payout 58%, >=2 17%, net median $85.9k; 2022+ pool .96/mo, gap 5.5 mo. $2,000 rule: .61/mo, gap 9.1 mo. Droughts are structural to stream x rules, not one bad path. Variants ($500): size x1.25 .85/mo gap 6.3; x1.5 .95/mo gap 5.8 mo; x2 1.12/mo gap 4.9; x2.5 1.25; x3 1.37/mo gap 3.8 mo (mo>=2 47%, net $207k) - linear scaling assumes contract caps (40 MNQ eval, 20/30/40 funded) do not bind and no cost of size; range-target sizing HURTS (.68/mo; scales down on the high-range days the algo makes its money on); event x1.5 (NFP/reaction, 2022+) +.04/mo (small, era-dependent). Only Lucid Flex tested; Apex/Topstep parameters unverified.

## Report 275 - size ladder in the FULL Lucid sim (MAE, caps, E2/F0 policies; u x k on all 8 models; per-leg cap 1.75 and 4.0 give identical results = not binding)
$500 rule payouts/mo | evals | net $: x1.0 .53 | 24 | $62.1k; x1.25 .55 | 24 | $68.5k; x1.5 .58 | 24 | $76.8k (worst12 $0); x2 .41 | 46 | $47.5k; x2.5 .42 | 61 | $50.2k; x3 .45 | 62 | $56.2k. $2,000 rule: x1.0 .58 | 17 | $89.7k (max gap 197d, worst12 $1,800) is the best; x1.25 .49, x1.5 .45, x2 .40. Report 274's linear scaling was WRONG: beyond x1.5 evals blow up (46-62 evals, 33-50 never paid), more resets eat the gain. Size is not the lever; the ceiling of this stream under these rules is about .55-.6 payouts a month. Real sim matches Rey's ".5 a month". Next lever: more uncorrelated streams / old-regime years, not size.

## Reports 276-277 - VWAP-deviation fade, VWAP crossings, opening range (declared; the apparent lead was a weighting artifact)
Setup: checkpoints 10:30..14:30, dev=(close-VWAP)/R20, fade 60 min, pts per contract, split train 2019-21 | test. Report 276 as first written averaged observations per DAY then across days: big-|dev| fade +4.5 | +5.4 (t 4.0 | 3.6), one-sided days (<=1 VWAP cross) +9.0 | +10.4 (t 5.1 | 5.6). Report 277 follow-ups looked contradictory (per-checkpoint means negative, tradeable stop/target version -1.4..-4.8 net, 90% of the "fades" were against the day's final direction). Cause: equal-weighting days after conditioning on later checkpoints = survivorship (a day that has reverted stops qualifying at later checkpoints, leaving only the early reverting observation; days that keep trending contribute several negative observations but one day-weight). Observation-weighted, day-clustered: all -0.5 | -0.6; big -1.1 | -1.9; big & gamma+ +0.1 | -1.2; big & cross<=1 -0.8 | -2.3; big & cross>=3 & gamma+ -0.05 | +1.9 (t .4). No lead. Lesson for future reports: aggregate per observation (or per trade) with day-clustered errors, never mean-of-day-means when qualification depends on the path.

## Report 278 - volatility + orderflow reversal: pre-declared score (V1 + V2 + U>=.5 + QQQ gamma+) on the 2,862 rejected-extreme events of report 264
Observation-weighted, day-clustered. p618 net pts train | test by score: 0: -1.8 | -0.7; 1: -5.0 | -6.3 (t -2.3 | -3.4); 2: -1.0 | -1.3; 3: -8.7 | +2.3; 4: +27.5 | +39.2 (n 8 | 8). Score>=2: -1.6 | +0.1 (n 142 | 374). The score does not work as a dose-response. Singles: gamma+ alone -3.9 | -6.0 (t -2.2 | -3.3) (rejected extremes on positive-gamma days are WORSE fades by themselves, hold 22% | 16%); U>=.5 -2.2 | -2.6. Only combination in the flag family that is positive both splits with a strong hold rate: V1 & V2 & gamma+: hold 50% | 53% (base 16% | 17%), p618 +16.8 | +32.7 (t 2.1 | 1.8), n 12 | 17 (29 trades in 8 years, ~3-4 a year, 1 of 10 cells tried). Verdict: a lead worth a declared forward test, not a leg (frequency too low to move payouts; real tick CVD exists only Dec 2024-Nov 2025). The vol-forecast component adds nothing beyond making the target and stop sizes sensible.

## Report 279 - the triple (volume building + absorption + own-GEX>0 at a rejected extreme) on 19 liquid names with their own GEX, 15m (41,937 events)
Hold rate (event extreme stays the session extreme) train | test: all events 13% | 14%; V1 28% | 27%; V2 27% | 26%; V1&V2 29% | 28%; V1&V2&G 30% | 29%; V1&V2&~G 27% | 28%. The doubling replicates in both splits across 19 names, so volume building / absorption at a rejected extreme really does mark the extreme about twice as often. Net R per trade (stop extreme +- .02 R20, target .618 R20, 2 bps cost): all -0.086 | -0.065 (t -4.2 | -3.8); V1&V2&G -0.105 | -0.103 (n 294 | 372, t -1.1 | -1.3); every cell negative. Gamma adds nothing on stocks (G vs ~G equal; 83% of name-days are G). The NQ V1&V2&G result (+16.8 | +32.7, 29 trades) is not supported by a wider sample (gamma part likely noise). The signal marks WHERE the extreme is but a tight-stop reversal cannot monetise a ~30% hold rate. Better use: as a filter on trend-leg entries/exits.

## Report 280 - volume-building / absorption extremes as a filter on main-algo entries (RA and V15a only: the legs with stored direction; M1/R1/RULES have none, ON+/ON-/S1 enter overnight/09:30)
Entry ALIGNED with a fresh session extreme (last bar before entry made the new high/low in the trade direction): 56-57% of trades; pts per trade flagged vs rest, train | test: +8.4 vs +8.1 | +12.3 vs +15.9 (t +0.0 | -0.5) = no effect. ALIGNED & V1: -6.8 vs +8.8 (n 13) | +19.5 vs +13.6 (n 36); ALIGNED & V2: diff -15.9 (t -2.1, n 34) | +14.3 (t +0.9, n 75): SIGN FLIPS between splits. Full F (rejection & V1 & V2): n 2 | 4 trades. No filter. The volume-building / absorption signal marks extremes (hold rate doubles, 19 names) but does not separate good from bad main-algo entries. Closed.

## Report 281 - exits that do not depend on the extreme holding (stops tight/.25/.5 R20/none x time 30-120 min, EOD, VWAP) on NQ rejected extremes, 2,999 events
Net pts per trade, train | test. V1&V2 (n 94 | 149): tight stop 60m +0.8 | -2.4; .25 R20 stop EOD +13.5 | -17.1; no stop EOD +11.1 | -27.9; VWAP +9.2 | -17.5 (test t -2.2). V1: similar (+9.7 | -12.6 at .25/EOD). V2: +14.2 | -15.8. All rejected extremes: -0.2..-6 train, -2..-15.5 test (t to -3.6). Wider stops / longer holds make the TEST worse in every cell; train improves to +9..+14 (n<=119, t<=1.5). Regime read: 2019-21 had enough rotation for volume-building fades to work with room; 2022+ trend years punish giving the extreme room (same blind-year story as the main algo, mirrored). Nothing positive in both splits. Tick-level CVD (only Dec 2024-Nov 2025, ~30 events) and algo-exit use (legs store no exit times) are not testable on current data.

## Report 282 - high-quality vector flips across 9 assets (NQ, QQQ, SPY, DIA, IWM, GLD, SLV, USO, TLT; 15m RTH, NQ-tuned vector, 2 bps)
Quality score = flow + ATRP top half + participation + daily-trend alignment (pre-declared, no fitting). Pooled mean % per trade train | test: all flips -0.001 | +0.003; score>=3 +0.040 (t1.1) | +0.014 (t0.5); score 4 -0.007 | -0.067. NQ is the only asset with a real flip edge: all flips +0.136% (t3.1) | +0.034% (t0.9); score>=3 +0.353% (t3.3) | +0.093% (t1.4). QQQ weaker same sign (+0.18 | +0.07); every other asset ~0 or negative (GLD test -0.064, t -3.6; SLV, TLT, IWM negative). Daily best-of across assets (score>=3, one trade a day): +0.013 | +0.035% (t 0.3 | 1.2). No multi-asset edge from the vector; it is an NQ/QQQ tool. No GEX exists for DIA/GLD/SPY/IWM (only QQQ options on disk), so gamma filters could not be run there; YM/GC futures themselves not on disk.

## Report 283 - fleet: N copies of the 8-model algo, staggered starts, full Lucid sim (per-account fees $90/eval + $100/mo)
$2,000 rule x1.0, start gap 40d: N=1 .58 payouts/mo, months>=1 47%, longest gap 197d, net $89.7k; N=2 1.17/mo, >=2 in 47% of months, gap 197d, net $179k; N=3 1.75/mo, gap 184d, $269k; N=4 2.34/mo, months>=1 52%, >=2 48%, gap 184d, net $359k, worst 12m paid $10.8k; N=5 2.92/mo. $500 rule x1.0 N=4 2.12/mo, gap 243d, $248k. Gap spacing 15-80d changes little (N=4 gap 80d: 2.27/mo, months>=1 59%, longest gap 167d). Payouts scale almost linearly with N (average 2/mo needs N~3.5), but copies share one trade stream, so payouts arrive in clumps: months with >=1 payout stay ~50%, months with >=2 ~ months with >=1, longest gap barely shortens (197 -> 184d). A fleet raises the average, not the regularity. Assumes the firm allows N accounts / copy trading (not verified).

## Report 284 - 2 accounts with the 8 models SPLIT (A: RA, R1, ON+, ON-; B: RULES, M1, V15a, S1) vs 2 identical copies
$2k rule: identical x2 1.17/mo, gap 197d, net $179k; split x1.0 0.50/mo, gap 365d, net $65k (A alone .20/mo, gap 671d; B alone .30/mo); sizing split accounts up (x1.5, x2) does not fix it. $500 rule: identical 1.05/mo, split 0.65/mo. The combined 8-model stream is what clears Lucid's rules; splitting models across accounts is much worse than copying the full set. Fleet = identical copies.

## Reports 285 + leg diagnosis (weak years 2018, 2019, 2023 vs the rest), base-size legs
Pts per trade by ex-post day type (2018-25): M1 MR -23.0 | MID -8.7 | TREND +36.3 ($-17.9k | -10.0k | +64.7k); V15a MR -24.5 | MID +0.6 | TREND +60.8 ($-6.8k | +2.5k | +54.5k); RA -2.2 | +7.3 | +25.7; RULES +0.9 | +2.4 | +3.5 (win rate 71-78%, the only all-weather leg); S1 +28.8 | +11.2 | +43.0; ON+ +2.2 | +9.1 | +6.6. Weak vs rest years, pts per trade: V15a +2.6 vs +19.3, S1 +0.8 vs +44.1, ON+ +0.5 vs +8.2, R1 +0.5 vs +8.7, RA +9.0 vs +12.0. MFE (max favourable excursion to the close) in weak years is about half: V15a 53 vs 118 pts, S1 66 vs 182, RA 71 vs 129; win rates similar (V15a 28% vs 32%), mean win 62 vs 142, mean loss -20 vs -40 (stops scale with ADR), capture of MFE about the same (V15a 32% vs 39%). So the weak years are SMALLER TRENDS, not an exit failure or more losing days. Full Lucid sim, size UP when prior-20d range is below the train median (156.7 pts), cap x1.25/1.5/2.0: $2k rule 0.58 | 0.59 | 0.60 payouts/mo vs 0.58 base, longest gap 383 | 239 | 358 vs 197 d; $500 rule 0.58 | 0.59 | 0.57 vs 0.53, net $69.5k | 71.2k | 66.8k vs 62.1k. Vol-targeting both ways (cap 1.5): 0.49 | 0.45/mo, worse. Low-range sizing is worth about +3-10% net, not a lever. Forward test on Feb-Jul 2026 NOT possible yet: options data ends 2025-12-15 and the leg tables (s6.pkl, uni_ifvg, tp168, ifvg_1m189, s1_trades162, nq_days) end 2026-01-30; a rebuild is a multi-report job.

## Report 286 - price-neutral view (Rey: NQ points scale with price; judge in return, not points)
NQ median open by year: 2018 6,982; 2019 7,686; 2020 10,268; 2021 14,528; 2022 12,437; 2023 14,824; 2024 19,137; 2025 22,915 (3.3x). RTH range in points 111 | 83 | 204 | 193 | 302 | 197 | 244 | 320 but in bps of price 161 | 111 | 208 | 135 | 237 | 141 | 127 | 147: the points trend is mostly price level. Mean bps per trade (V15a 0.8 | -2.3 | 10.3 | 8.9 | 17.6 | 8.0 | 13.4 | 12.5; S1 3.1 | -7.1 | -1.4 | 10.8 | 49.8 | 3.1 | -3.3 | 43.2). Algo return in bps of a 10-MNQ notional (price-neutral) by year: 984 | 707 | 846 | 956 | 1,647 | 1,131 | 809 | 1,203 (2018 is 82% of 2025, not 25%). In dollars 2018-19 look weak ($13.8k | $10.6k vs $54.9k in 2025) mainly because fixed 10 MNQ was 1/3 of the notional. Full Lucid sim, contracts scaled by P_ref/price: ref 12.4k: $500 rule 0.60/mo (base 0.53), 21 evals (base 24), nopay 3 (8), net $64.8k (62.1k); $2k rule 0.51 (0.58), net $77.3k (89.7k), longest gap 385 d. ref 22.9k (what constant notional at 2025 price means for all years): 0.45/mo, 32 evals, 21 nopay, net $48-65k, longest gap 500 d. Reading: held at today's price level, the same stream blows more evals (the $2,000 MLL is fixed while notional risk 3.3x); fixed contracts made 2018-21 safer in dollars and thinner in payouts. Fixed contracts at 25k price carry more dollar risk per trade than the 2018-21 backtest did.

## Report 294 (Nov 18 2026): screener options-ratio controls + giveback. (A) Trade return vs log ratio, ratio>=1.5 universe: 0.00/+0.06%% per sd (t<1.3), zero with controls; ratio corr 0.62 with log $volume, and $volume carries the test-period return (+0.21%%/sd, t 2.7). By bucket: ratio 1.0-1.5 +0.04/+0.13%%, >1.5 +0.24-0.48%%, flat above 1.5. => threshold/liquidity effect, not speculation; slot-order gain comes from crowded bars. (B) 9,312 trades Dec 2023+: mean MFE 3.6%%, giveback 3.35%%, but ATR trails (2/3/4 ATR) and 50%% MFE lock change mean by -0.03..+0.01 pts: no fix. Flip-quality from vector internals not testable here (15m bars on Mac).

K sensitivity: K=1 ~same, K=3 32.4, K=5 26.7 (worse than live) -> not robust. Ablation: TREND-only 36.6/-10.8, HI52-only 35.7/-10.9, SIZE-only 34.7, MOM-only 32.3 (worse than live). ## Reports 301-303 (Nov 18 2026): is there ONE factor behind the boosted signals? Answer: no. 301: seven point-in-time signals (ratio pct, $vol, 12-1 mom, >200d, near 52w high, 20d mom, rel-strength) form clusters (ratio+$vol 0.47; trend+52wH+12-1 0.34-0.67; 20d mom = rel-strength 1.00), PC1 35% of variance, PC1 has NO stable return (within-bar FE t -0.7 train, +1.8 test). Return decomposed into beta x SPY-over-hold + rest: alpha part spread ~0.2pp across signal counts. 302: variance budget (adj R2 of flip return): name 0%, entry-week 6-10% (in-sample), entry bar 4-6%; 56-67% of trades share a bar with another candidate. Hit rate ~31% regardless of signal count; signals shrink avg LOSS (train -1.58% -> -1.05%), not hit rate. Index variance-ratio (5d/1d, 120d): LOW VR (choppy tape) -> better flips in both halves (0.63/0.49 vs 0.08/0.30, t -2.3/-0.9), OPPOSITE of the declared direction -> lead only (flip = reversal entry after a bear leg). 303: trend and 52w-high cut sigma (t -10 to -19, same sign both halves) with ~0 return effect; ratio/$vol RAISE sigma in test (t +8) and return (+2.6) = megacap regime. Mean return/trade flat across vol quintiles (0.2-0.4%) while std 2.6% -> 5.1%. A plain low-vol tilt (ratio rank - 5 x low-vol pct) reproduces report 300: 24.0/35.3, DD -13.0/-10.9 vs boost 23.1/35.8, DD -13.0/-10.9; K=10/20 does not (32.0, 29.4). Inverse-vol sizing = de-levering: 20.9/28.8 (DD -11.7/-13.2, vol 6.2/6.0) vs live 22.9/33.4 (-13.0/-16.2, 7.3/6.8); Calmar ~+2%/+6%. Conclusion: the boost is a risk (sigma) selector, not a return predictor; admission rule for future signals: same sign both halves, |t|>2 in one, stated failure regime.

## Reports 305-309 (Nov 18 2026): the NQ stack equation and where it stops. Identity: pnl = capture x range x efficiency. Range is forecastable (HAR+IV R2 .39-.56; first-hour volume rho +.42/+.32), day type is not. 305 megacap coherence (C1h, Cday): AUC .52-.53, range rho +.07 -> dead as day-type predictor. 306 generic no-parameter 15m ORB (stop .20 ADR14, hold to close, 1.5bp): NQ -3.1|+1.4 bp/trade, gold -1.7|-1.5, silver -1.8|-3.9, copper -3.0|-3.6 (t -3.0 test), crude -1.5|-0.5; daily corr ~0 -> the trend-capture mechanism does not generalise to commodities, NQ marginal. 307 pts/day by day type (train|test): all 8 models rotation -11.6|-19.8, mid -4.2|-4.3, trend +50.0|+83.1; capture on trend days by year .42 .31 .36 .33 .40 .19 .32 .47 .20 (2018-26). 308 share of trend move done by 10:30 rose .29-.41 (2018-24) -> .46 (2025) .50 (2026); but 2025 was front-loaded and captured best, so timing alone does not explain 2026 (legs store no entry times). 309 opening-drive continuation (10:30, first-hour move >= k ADR, top-third efficiency, stop .25 ADR, 1pt cost): k=.35 +7.7 (t1.7,n192)|+12.9 (t2.4,n377); fade placebo -5.2|-3.6; positive 8 of 9 years but 2026 -34 pts (n47, 28% win); six cells tried; daily corr with stack .18 -> candidate leg, not validated. 2026 stack Feb-Oct +531 pts (1.8/day): ON+ +384, RA +251, R1 +82, V15a -113, ON- -66. Full write-up: Docs artifact "What the data says: NQ stack and screener, model by model". Open: why capture fell in 2026; index-futures and 5m screener pulls pending.

## Report 310 (Nov 18 2026): lead 1, capture by entry time. Legs DO store entry minute m (ET) for RA/M1/R1/V15a/RULES/S1 (ON legs m=-1); the earlier "no entry times" note was wrong. Hypothesis (later entries find less move after front-loading) FALSIFIED: no entry-bucket decay for M1/V15a/RULES; RA opening legs flat. Only visible pockets: M1 <=10:00 2026 -24 pts/trade (n36, one year), V15a 11:30-13:30 = -0.1|-4.1 pts/trade train|test (t -0.5, n39|101), -52 (2025) and -33 (2026) after +46 in 2024, so noise-level and driven by few trades. ## Report 311 (Nov 18 2026): 2026 missed trend days. Hypothesis (capture drop sits in trend days where the first hour fades the final direction) FALSIFIED: such REV days are only 5 of 56 trend days in 2026 (6-19 per year earlier); on CONT days (91% of trend days) capture fell .35 (2025) -> .19 (2026) with stack pts/day still +69.7 (2023: +44.6, 2024: +78.5, 2025: +99.3) because |net| in points kept growing. Scale-free view (all 8 models, pts/ADR14 per day): 2018 .090, 2019 .027, 2020 .091, 2021 .072, 2022 .084, 2023 .068, 2024 .083, 2025 .137, 2026 .020 -> 2026 is the worst year of the sample in ADR units, ADR bps (151) is normal so it is NOT a low-vol year; 2019 is the only comparable year. Trend-day supply: 56 days, win 61%, |net|/ADR .90 (lowest of sample). Read: 2026 weakness = legs capturing less of normal-size trend days, concentrated in V15a/RULES/ON- (see 2026 stack by model), not entry timing, not reversal days, not low volatility.

## Reports 312-313 (Nov 18 2026): FIRST MACRO/FLOW LAYER, CTA trend-flow proxy (BofA-monitor style, built from our own futures: 3 windows x 4 stops = 12 models per market, NQ/ES/TLT/crude/gold/copper, all features lagged one day). Prompted by Rey's bank papers (BofA CTA monitor, GS flow notes) and the "macro/fundamentals = thesis, TA = entry" point. 312 pre-declared H1 (CTA position/flow -> next-day range): FALSIFIED (|rho| < .06 for level, trigger distance, stopped-count; dpos rho -.13|-.10 is the leverage effect, corr with yesterday's return .29). H2 level of CTA position -> algo pts: nothing (low/mid/high +12.9/-3.2/+11.0 train). EXPLORATORY find: the SIGN of yesterday's CTA-proxy NQ position change sorts next-day algo ALL pts/day: after increase +31.1|+52.8 (n79|205), after decrease -26.9|-16.4 (n75|186), unchanged +13.4|+21.5. Same ordering in 9 of 9 years for the increase side and 7 of 9 for the decrease side; 118 episodes. 313 robustness: ES dpos +43.8|+50.7 up, -10.7|-12.7 down; alt windows 10/42/252 + stops 2-5 +28.6|+48.7 vs -13.4|-15.6; single windows 21/63/126 all keep ordering (63d test down +1.2); placebos (lag 7, shuffled) show no ordering. Tilt x1.5 after increases, x0.5 after decreases: mean/std .113->.131 train, .130->.144 test; std up 9-12%, worst day -452 -> -508. NOT yet a rule: (1) sign-of-change was chosen after terciles degenerated, (2) next-day NQ drifts slightly AGAINST the flow (net/ADR -0.10 after increases in test) so mechanism for why the algo gains is unproven, (3) no Lucid sim yet, (4) data ends 2026-08-14 (d305). Next: Lucid size-tilt sim, why the algo wins on those days (leg-level, which legs/directions), out-of-sample on Aug-Nov 2026.

## Report 315 + CTA-lead follow-ups (Nov 18 2026): literature leads and the screener page. (A) Gao-Han-Li-Zhou 2018 intraday momentum on NQ RTH 5m (first half-hour sign -> trade 15:30-16:00, 1pt cost): train -1.31 pts/trade (t-0.8, n749), test -2.19 (t-2.6, n2445); overnight+first-half-hour -3.19|-1.40; placebo (yesterday's first half-hour) -2.50|-0.13; by year -0.1 to -6.1 in 2018, 2021-24, 2026, +1.8 only 2025. The published effect (1993-2013) is absent and if anything reversed (closing fade ~+1.2 pts gross), consistent with Kurth-Eisler-Rej-Bouchaud arXiv 2607.01550 (fast trend Sharpe .84 -> .12 after 2009 in small-tick/thin-book contracts; loss concentrated on large-return days). No rule. (B) b302 market-state terciles on flip ret (train|test): breadth low +0.05|+0.34%, mid +0.53|+0.28%, high +0.28|+0.13% (no ordering, fails same-sign admission); SPY>200d low +0.09|+0.84, high +0.38|+0.21 (sign flips). Screener-page breadth and trend-state features are NOT admitted. CTA lead follow-ups (312/313): month-block bootstrap of up-minus-down ALL pts/day: train +58.1 (95% CI 23 to 100), test +69.2 (37 to 105), P(<=0) < .001 both; survives a x60 multiple-testing haircut. Concentration: ON- carries it (after CTA increase +12.7|+17.4 pts/day, after decrease -12.6|-18.9, flat +2.8|+1.0), M1 +12.9|+7.4 vs -5.4|-2.4, V15a +5.4|+11.8 vs -2.6|+1.2; RA/R1/RULES/S1 ~0. Not only yesterday's direction: cells by yesterday-tercile x dpos sign keep ordering in 3 of 4 (y-down and dpos>0 is largest +87.8|+69.9 but n7|35; y-up dpos<0 flips in test n17). Mechanism unproven; likely an ON- (overnight short) gate. Admission rule for next step: Lucid size-tilt on ON-/M1/V15a only, pre-registered x1.5/x0.5, same-sign both eras.

## Report 325 (Nov 18 2026): Rey's concern that capping per-sector hurts in growth (semis/tech, "the more the better"). Growth = tech+semi+comm (declared before running). Slot book CAGR/DD 2019-22 | 2023-26: live 22.9/-13.0 | 33.4/-16.2; cap 3 all sectors 20.2/-13.0 | 36.9/-10.6; cap 3 NON-growth only (growth uncapped) 20.2/-13.0 | 36.7/-10.6 (same as all-sector cap); cap 3 on growth only 22.9/-13.0 | 33.6/-16.2 (= live: growth cap never binds); cap 2 non-growth only 20.4 | 34.3 (DD -15.1); ranker alone 23.1 | 37.3 (DD -10.9); ranker + non-growth cap 3 19.9 | 34.5 (DD -16.6). All the cap's effect comes from non-growth sectors (cons 22% of flips, fin, indu, stap). Mechanism evidence (all candidate flips, ret % by same-sector flips already open, train | test): growth 0 open +0.32|+0.36, 1-2 open +0.46|+0.56 (concentration pays: +0.14|+0.20 pp), 3+ open -0.23 (n58)|+0.29 (n1272); non-growth 0 open +0.12|+0.05, 1-2 open +0.38|+0.23, 3+ open +0.02|+0.10 (3rd+ name adds nothing: -0.36|-0.13 pp vs 1-2 open). Candidate rule with a stated reason: cap 3 open names per NON-growth sector, growth uncapped (growth flips co-move and pay; non-growth flips cluster on shared macro/consumer news and the third+ adds no return). Caveat: CAGR cost 2.7pp in 2019-22, +3.3pp in 2023-26; drawdown benefit (-16.2 -> -10.6) appears only in 2023-26; ranker and cap do not stack.

All features ex-ante, tercile cuts from train 2019-21, directions fixed before running, test = 2018 + 2022-26, 0.75 pt cost. Base R/trade train +0.146 (t+1.9) | test +0.193 (t+3.3). V2 ATRP R train +0.156/+0.175/+0.106, test +0.345/+0.132/+0.143; points high vs low flip sign -> no structure. V3 VIX: no monotonic pattern (mid best in train, low best in test). G1 GAP ALIGNMENT (break in the direction of the overnight gap): WITH +0.264R (t+2.2, n330) | +0.272R (t+3.2, n663) vs AGAINST +0.044 (t+0.5) | +0.120 (t+1.5); points WITH +7.2 | +12.7 vs AGAINST -2.3 | +5.9; with-minus-against difference +0.220R (t+1.4) | +0.151R (t+1.3) (each half alone not significant, same sign); 46-48% of days aligned. Large gap (|gap|/ADR above train median) AND aligned: +0.411R (t+2.4, n168) | +0.435R (t+3.2, n302) vs aligned small gap +0.112 | +0.135 (second-stage cut, adds a degree of freedom). By year WITH the gap: 2018 +0.52, 2019 +0.24, 2020 +0.12, 2021 +0.44, 2022 +0.33, 2023 +0.16, 2024 +0.31, 2025 +0.03, 2026 +0.26 (AGAINST 2026 -0.20).
