Caveats: daily n small (extension n=7), earnings via 8-K 2.02 accept time, valuation from sec_q TTM revenue (Q4 = FY - Q1..Q3), shares latest, sector proxy = equal-weight peers ex self (hlth/energy have a single peer), wide_T starts 2019 (no 2018 in test).

## Report 457 (2026-11-24): do other greeks / option flow / vol features improve the flip picks beyond the net-gamma sign; short-dated (<=2 DTE) vs longer-dated gamma
Data: xs/build_opt_features.py -> data/theta_feat2 (gex, short/long gamma, vanna, charm, vega, DEX, put/call OI + volume, ATM IV 30d, term, skew) for 12 names; flips = wide_T (S&P, ratio >= 1.5) on the 8 stock names with theta (AAPL AMD AMZN COST META MSFT NVDA TSLA; 3,228 15m + 1,321 1h flips); every feature shifted one day (prior close). 9 declared tests x 2 timeframes = 18; PASS = |t|>=2 in the declared direction, same sign train/test.
Result 1 of 18: F4 put/call VOLUME top third beats bottom third at 15m: +67bp t 2.48 (train +56 t 1.44, test +74 t 2.00); holds inside neg-gamma (+123bp t 2.21) and pos-gamma (+70bp t 1.81); 1h wrong sign (-20bp t -0.31). Chance expectation ~1 in 18; unconfirmed.
Declared direction failed with significant OPPOSITE sign: F8 DEX high beats low is -71bp t -2.74 at 15m (train -107, test -50; low-DEX flips are better); F6 term inverted beats not is -91bp t -2.59 at 1h. Others: vanna, charm, IV rank, skew no effect (|t| < 1.6).
F1 baseline net gamma < 0 on this 8-name wide_T set: +25bp t 1.22 (15m), +21bp t 0.48 (1h) - weaker than the 451-455 sets (pooled means neg 36 vs pos 50 at 15m, i.e. month-paired and pooled disagree).
F9 (0DTE-ish vs regular): flips where SHORT-dated (<=2 DTE) gamma is negative vs where only the longer-dated gamma is negative: 1h +181bp t 1.88 (test t 2.16, train t 0.27, n 127/204); 15m -26bp t -0.66. Inconsistent across timeframes; a lead on 1h only. True 0DTE needs intraday option data (EOD files only reach <=2 DTE).
Caveats: 8 names; META from 2021-09; SPY/QQQ/IWM/DIA not in the flip set; features cross-name percentile ranks use each name's own trailing 252d; no split adjustment needed for ranks and signs.

### Report 457 rerun on 23 names (supersedes the 8-name result above)
Added theta features for GOOGL DIS ADBE HD TGT WMT CAT CRM UPS JPM ORCL MCD IBM UNH SBUX (23 names with flips + theta; 6,977 15m and 2,891 1h flips; GOOGL theta ends 2022-03). Names in the live flip set without raw theta on the Mac (BA GS BKNG PYPL FDX WYNN CMG NFLX ENPH AVGO LRCX ...) are still missing.
PASS (declared direction, |t|>=2, same sign train/test): F4 put/call VOLUME high beats low at 15m +47bp t 3.25 (train +52 t 1.89, test +44 t 2.68; inside neg-gamma +55 t 1.65, inside pos-gamma +62 t 2.69; 1h -13bp t -0.39). F7 skew high beats low at 15m +66bp t 2.58 (train +124 t 2.80, test +29 t 0.96; vanishes inside neg-gamma +14 t 0.38). F3 charm high beats low at 1h +81bp t 2.55 (train t 1.62, test t 1.99; 15m +17 t 1.29). 3 of 18; Bonferroni (18 tests) leaves only F4 15m.
Fails: F1 net gamma<0 vs >=0 is NOT significant on this set (+13bp t 0.95 15m, +19bp t 0.72 1h; by era 15m +4/+7/+5/+20bp); F9 short-dated vs longer-dated gamma collapsed (15m +12bp t 0.49, 1h +31bp t 0.74) - the 8-name 1h lead was small-sample noise; F2 vanna, F5 IV rank, F8 DEX (15m -32bp t -1.70) no pass; F6 term inverted 1h -47bp t -2.59 (wrong direction, test t -2.52).
Reading: of the options features tested, put/call volume (hedging demand in the name; mechanism: fear-driven lows revert) is the only one that survives multiple-testing, at 15m only; the net-gamma sign did not replicate on the wider 23-name ratio-gated flip set.

## Report 458 (2026-11-24): separate signal models on the options-ratio flip universe (23 names with theta so far)
Models: A negative gamma (prior-close gex<0), B put/call volume rank top third (15m only), C skew rank top third, D charm rank top third; plus unions. Month-clustered t vs 0; lift = vs all other gated flips of the same timeframe (month-paired). Hold = clock hours entry->exit (vector flips hold days, so per-hour figures are not meaningful).
15m: A n 2425 +46bp t 3.45 (train +47 t 2.1, test +45 t 2.7; lift +13bp t 0.95; eras 15/67/-9/30); B n 2656 +66bp t 5.30 (train +90 t 4.4, test +52 t 3.3; lift +38bp t 3.13; eras 50/80/10/42; 51% overlap with A); C n 2447 +74bp t 3.30 (train +128 t 3.2, test +40 t 1.5; lift +60bp t 2.64; eras 67/78/1/42); D n 1750 +50bp t 4.11 (lift +10bp t 0.84); B|C|D n 4296 +57bp t 5.29 (lift +51bp t 4.66); A|B|C|D n 4727 +53bp.
1h: A n 704 +70bp t 2.45 (train +122, test +44 t 1.2; lift +19bp t 0.72); C n 1055 +80bp t 3.48 (lift +40bp t 1.54); D n 616 +113bp t 3.52 (train +127 t 2.9, test +105 t 2.4; lift +65bp t 2.20; eras 123/133/2/74); B|C|D n 1764 +75bp t 3.97.
2022 is weak or flat for every model except 1h charm/skew's -10 to +2. Base rate on this set: all gated flips +31.8bp (15m), +49.6bp (1h).
Download blocker: Rey's Theta Terminal (127.0.0.1:25503) is not reachable from the cloud session; the full per-contract pull script (fetch_theta_options.py) is not in the repo. 31 live-set names still have no raw theta (BA GS BKNG PYPL FDX WYNN CMG NFLX ENPH AVGO LRCX SMCI LLY PANW MU CRWD LMT PLTR C OXY COIN ABNB HOOD LULU APP PENN CVNA QCOM DLTR GEV UBER).

## Report 459 (2026-11-24): portfolio books from the signal models (23 names, 6 slots x 1/6, realized P&L, compounding, 2019-01..2026-10, first flip takes a free slot, one position per name)
15m: MAIN all gated flips CAGR +28.2% maxDD -14.9% (3,828 trades); A negative gamma +13.0% DD -12.9% (2,083); B put/call vol +21.8% DD -18.0%; C skew +17.4% DD -16.9%; D charm +9.5% DD -14.3%; B|C|D +25.3% DD -17.4%; A + B|C|D +21.2% DD -18.3% (3,257).
1h: MAIN +16.1% DD -21.5%; A +6.4% DD -18.6%; C skew +6.2% DD -12.9%; D charm +10.1% DD -11.6%; B|C|D (skew+charm on 1h) +12.0% DD -14.3%; A + C|D +11.1% DD -22.3%.
Reading: adding the models to the negative-gamma book roughly doubles its CAGR, but trading every gated flip on these 23 names beats every filtered book - negative gamma is the weakest filter here (it drops profitable trades). Caveats: universe = live-screener names (possible hindsight selection), open trades not marked (drawdowns understated), 23 of 54 names.
Live wiring: option_models.py (NEG/PCV/SKW/CHM flags) posts to the model channel with the models named; build_opt_features.py now also writes charm by days-to-expiry (<=7, 8-30, >30) and all-expiry put/call volume; the charm-by-expiry test is not run yet.

## Report 460 (2026-11-24): EMA / SMA trend filters (20/50/100/200 x 15m/1h/daily) on the options-ratio gated flips (54 live names; 12,366 15m and 5,081 1h flips)
Declared: price ABOVE the MA beats below (trend-aligned). 48 tests; PASS needs t >= 3.3 (Bonferroni) in the declared direction and same sign in train and test. PASS: none. Every above-minus-below difference is negative: below-the-MA flips are as good or better.
Best, opposite to the declared direction: 15m flips vs the 1h SMA 100 -29bp t -3.55 (train -39 t -2.6, test -23 t -2.4); 15m SMA 200 -21bp t -2.32; 15m EMA 200 -24bp t -2.67; 1h SMA 20 -21bp t -2.51. For 1h flips nothing reaches |t| 2.1 except 1h EMA 20 -50bp t -1.95.
Win rate is not moved by any MA filter (30-33% vs 31-32%). Models vs base win rate on the 23 names: 15m base 32.2%, NEG 32.4%, PCV 33.5%, SKW 34.8%, CHM 33.4%; 1h base 35.6%, NEG 36.5%, SKW 36.6%, CHM 36.4%; median trade stays negative (-51 to -60bp at 15m) - the edge is the right tail, not a higher hit rate.
Live: 1h SMA 100 drawn as a white dashed line on the 15m alert chart (context; below it did better).

## Report 461 (2026-11-24): signal models (NEG/PCV/SKW/CHM) on the Fantasy perps prop, High tier (reports 443/444 machinery, same 23 names, Gf 1)
Models cannot add trades (they only fire on flips already in the G20 gated book); they select. Trades/month: base 39, NEG 13, PCV 14, SKW 12, CHM 8, ANY 27.
Ge 1: base passed by 30/60/90d 10/29/45%, first payout by 90d 21%, breach 21%, net/attempt +655 (18-21 +1,216, 22+ +215). ANY 7/17/26%, payout90 13%, breach 14%, net +724 (22+ +186). PCV 3/9/15%, breach 8%, net +363 (22+ +34). NEG, SKW, CHM lower still. Fewer trades = fewer filled slots = slower to the +10% target; the lower breach does not pay for it. Fails the declared bar (beat base net in 22+ and payout by 90d >= 25%): none does. Selection filters do not help the perps prop; the full gated book stays best.

## Report 462 (2026-11-24): HMM on options features (put/call vol, skew, charm, IV, gamma) applied to vector flips, 23 names
Gaussian HMM fit on 2019-21 days only, forward-filtered states (no look-ahead), flip state = prior-close day. Chosen = best train state; PASS needs test spread t >= 2, same sign, circular-shift null p < .05. Results: K=3 15m train +81bp t 3.07, TEST -7bp t -0.27, null p .71; K=3 1h train +99bp, TEST -36bp, p .94; K=2 15m train +37bp, TEST +4bp, p .43; K=2 1h TEST -33bp, p .92. No pass. States are just "high fear / low fear" (state 1: pc_vol rank .58, skew .64, IV .58, gz ~0; state 0: low ranks, dealers long gamma): the lift in train is the 2019-21 put/call volume effect that is weaker after 2021 (22: -0 vs -28). The HMM adds nothing over the plain put/call volume rank.

## Report 463 (2026-11-24): quarterly fundamentals + 12-month macro, simple models, to pick low-priced vector flips that run >=10% (xs/report_463_lowcap_fund_macro_flips.py)
structure_small flips 2019-2026Q1 with SEC facts (357 names), entry px < $10 n 1,857 (train 2019-21 517, test 2022+ 1,340); px < $20 n 3,776. Features PIT (facts filed <= flip date): rev_yoy, ni_margin, loss, runway, debt/assets, equity/assets, dilution, size; macro: 10y 12m change, curve, dollar/oil 12m, VIX (HY spreads dropped, FRED only ~3y). Logit M0 OS, M1 FUND, M2 FUND+MACRO, M3 OS+FUND+MACRO, HGB depth 2; frozen from train.
Test AUC for r>=10% (px<$10): OS .614, FUND .559, FUND+MACRO .614, OS+FUND+MACRO .621, HGB .612. FUND alone is weak (.56-.59, t 0.7-1.6). Adding fundamentals+macro to OS: +.007 AUC at px<$10 (not the +.02 bar), and -.03 at px<$20 for r>=30%. Top-quintile P(>=10%) 27-35% vs 14-15% rest but month-paired t only 1.2-1.6 (n 77-200). Top-10-flips-per-month basket: hit 19-20% vs base 16%, mean +5 to +10%, MEDIAN -8 to -10% (win 23-40%). Only one nominal significance: OS+FUND+MACRO r>=30% at px<$20 (t 2.90) but its AUC (.635) is BELOW OS-only (.665).
READ: PASS fails. Quarterly fundamentals add nothing beyond the technical outlier score (consistent with reports 326-327, 329); macro features act as regime shifters (the train-frozen quintile cut moves: top-quintile n swings 77 to 1,391), with ~7 independent annual observations they cannot be fitted. The big-move edge in small caps stays in the right tail (mean up, median down), not in a higher hit rate. Test has one era only (2022-26Q1), so the era-consistency leg of the pass rule cannot be evaluated.

## Report 464 (2026-11-24): flips with a reason to move - 8-K deals/events, insider buys, sector-specific macro tailwind (xs/report_464_catalyst_flips.py)
structure_small flips 2019-2026Q1, px < $10 (n 3,863; base P(r>=10%) 18.3%, median -6.3%, win 26%) and < $20 (8,408). 8-K flags (7-day window, known by the flip day): DEAL (items 1.01/2.01) n138: P(>=10%) 26% vs 18%, mean +18.9% vs +8.7%, higher in EVERY era (32v21, 23v15, 24v17), month-paired t 0.78 -> lead, not a pass. INS (insider open-market buy 30d) n279: P 21% vs 18%, mean +18.2 vs +8.4, eras mixed. EARN (2.02): P 23% vs 18% but mean diff -4.2pp, 2022 equal. PR (7.01/8.01) no effect. NEG control (3.02/1.02/1.03/2.05 dilution/termination/bankruptcy) n56: P 25% vs 18%, NOT worse -> the 8-K item flags are event flags, not good-news flags (small-cap dilution flips run too). Sector macro tailwind (oil/gold-dollar/curve/XLV/XLK/10y by SIC group) n1,550: 19.7% vs 18.4%, mean 8.3 vs 9.6 -> nothing. Combined reason gate (DEAL|INS|TAIL) keeps 46% of flips: P 20.3% vs 16.6%, mean +9.6 vs +8.6, t -0.6: does not improve the flips taken. No flag passes (every era + t >= 2.2). Missing: news content (deal size, counterparty, headline) - the item code says an agreement exists, not that it is good; px<$20 same picture (DEAL 22.8 vs 16.4, t 0.9).

## Report 465 (2026-11-24): what do the big low-priced movers have in common? 62 features in 7 declared families on 8,408 daily bull flips (px < $20, 2019-2026Q1; train 2019-21 | test 2022+; BIG = flip return >= +30%: 12.9% | 7.7%)
PASS rule: same side of .5 in both halves, |AUC-.5| >= .04 in both, test z >= 3.3. BIG>=30% AUC train | test: ATRP .699 | .660, F11 (distance to level) .664 | .654, outlier score OS .674 | .648, EXT .644 | .621, turnover LTURN .596 | .591, market cap LMC .393 | .412 and price LPX .428 | .413 (smaller/cheaper = more), VR .541 | .578, volume surge VOLX .543 | .576, range/ATR F2 .556 | .575, cash runway (log) .376 | .406 (SHORT runway = more), net margin .357 | .414 (loss-makers = more), curve .591 | .558 (macro, regime), F4 (close/52w high) .336 | .433 (far below the high = more). Same set for r>=10% (best: F11 .655 | .604, OS, ATRP, runway, VOLX).
NOT prevalent: sector rotation (best sect_rs20 .496 | .484, z -1.0; rank20 .473 | .518), deals/news (n8k30 .530 | .534 z 2.1 fails; DEAL/PR/EARN/NEG/INS none), earnings 8-K (r>=10%: .524 | .528 z 2.5), dilution FLIPS sign, oil_12m flips, overnight gap F3 not in the top.
READ: the big movers are the volatile, small, cheap, high-turnover names with a volume surge, and the cash-short loss-makers; the scan finds magnitude (who CAN move), not who is a good bet: reports 167/200 show the top of the same score has mean up and median down. Reasons-to-move (sector rotation, deals, news) do not separate big movers in this data; macro curve is regime-dependent (~7 independent years). Pre-market volume is not in any data we hold.

## Report 466 (2026-11-24): a group of low-priced flips with a high chance of a >=10% move (xs/report_466_high_prob_group.py)
Six-feature rank score frozen on train 2019-21 (ATRP, F11 distance to level, VOLX volume surge, LTURN turnover, short cash runway, small market cap; signs from report 465), tested 2022+. Base test: REAL (r >= +10%) 14.9%, REACH (peak >= +10% before the vector exit) 44.6%, median -5.9%. px < $10 top 19% / 9% / 5% (135 / 70 / 30 flips a year): REACH 66.5 / 70.0 / 72.2%, REAL 26.5 / 26.3 / 27.8%, mean +12.7 / +16.7 / +24.4%, median -11.5 / -13.2 / -16.2%, win ~30%; same in every year 2022-26 (REACH 61-71%, REAL 21-35%). OS top 10% is equal (REACH 70.6, REAL 25.2). Top 10 flips per month: REACH 63%, REAL 23%. Bar "REAL >= 40% in both eras" fails; the high-probability group exists only for REACH.
Side check (not a rule: a +10% sell on a vector flip is a layered target, against Rey's rule): selling at +10% hits 70% of the top-10% group but the 30% that miss lose -20% on average (median -19%): mean +0.9% (slip 0), +0.2% (1%), -0.5% (2%); 2022 -2.6%, 2023-26 +1.4%. The vector exit gives back the move; a fixed target does not fix expectancy.

## Report 467 (2026-11-24): ATRP relative to a HAR forecast, and other vol-state features (xs/report_467_har_vol_features.py; daily OHLCV of the 1,656-name wide universe)
HAR on Parkinson range variance, pooled OLS fitted on 2019-21 (coef d .25, w .31, m .38; R2 .64). AUC train | test for BIG (>=+30%): ATRP .699 | .660, HARv .693 | .663, GK22 .690 | .652 (same information as ATRP), ATRP/HAR .456 | .426 (LOWER = more movers: high expected range, current ATR at or below it), SURP (today's range / yesterday's forecast) .553 | .575, overnight gap activity GAPABS20 .669 | .636, VOV .551 | .562; REACH (peak >= +10%): ATRP .660 | .637, ATRP/HAR .442 | .416, SURP .578 | .586, GAPABS20 .613 | .600.
Stage 2 (declared after stage 1): adding GAPABS20 / SURP / ATRP_HAR(-) / VOV to the five-feature score does NOT improve the top-10% group (px<10, test 2022+): ATRP alone n163 REAL 29% REACH 69% med -13.4%; base5 27% / 69% / -12.7%; +GAPABS20 28% / 69% / -12.4%; +SURP 27% / 70%; +ATRP_HAR 26% / 70%; +VOV 25% / 67%; all four 25% / 67% / -13.7%. REACH tops out around 67-70% and REAL ~25-29% whatever the score; the median stays -12 to -14%. The vol-state features describe the same names as ATRP.

## 468 (2026-11-24) time-to-+10%, hold length, concentration of the high-probability low-price group
Group = top 10% base5 score (ATRP,F11,VOLX,LTURN,LMC), 2022+ test, trades reconstructed from daily OHLCV (98-100% reconcile).
- px<10 (n298): 69% touch +10%; of ALL flips 19% by day1, 32% d2, 40% d3, 43% d5, 55% d10, 63% d20; median 3d to reach (p75 9, p90 19). px<20 (n601): 67%; 15/26/34/40/52/60%; median 3d (p90 22). Train matches (70%/69%, median 4-5d).
- Non-reachers (31-33%) are cut by the vector exit at median 8d (p90 27) = only 6-7% of capital-days. The long holds are the REACHERS riding the vector (median 55-68d). Selling at +10% else vector exit: avg hold 8-9d vs 55-60d (83-86% capital-days saved).
- Concentration (px<10, 2022-25): 37/66/91/95 flips, 29-73 names, top-5 names 17-27%, top month 15-27%, SIC 28 (chem/pharma/biotech) 30-41% every year. 2026 has only 9 flips (data ends Q1) - too thin to judge. All-flips-held-to-vector concurrency avg 5->22 open (max 40); sell-at-+10% brings it to ~3-7.
Caveat: +10% touch is intraday high, not guaranteed fill; flips/yr ~90 (px<10) / ~185 (px<20).

## 469 (2026-11-24) real-world reasons INSIDE the high-vol group, px<20 / <10 / <5 (all FAIL)
Base group = top 30% base5 score (REACH>=10% already 60% / 63% / 68% test; REAL 21 / 24 / 26%). SEC facts extended to 1,017 names (93% of group covered). Features: RUNWAY (cash / monthly operating burn; short = more), SQ (burning & <12m), RAISE90 (8-K 3.02), SECMAC (sector macro driver favourable), DEAL, INS, EV5, GATE2 (any reason). Rule: same side train/test, |AUC-.5|>=.04 both, test z>=3.3.
- NOTHING passes in any bucket or target. Best test AUCs: GATE2 on REAL .543/.546 (z 2.4/1.9), RUNWAY_SHORT on REACH .527 (train .503/.474, wrong side in px<10 train), all others .50-.52.
- DEAL (+6pp REACH at <20, +7 at <10) and INS (+7 / +10pp) lead in 2019-21 and 2023-26 but INS REACH was BELOW no-flag in 2022 in all three buckets (n 23/11/5); flags are 4-9% of group. Leads only, as in 67b/464.
- Short runway is not the catalyst: SQ REACH 62% v 60% (<20), 64 v 63 (<10), 66 v 72 (<5, wrong sign). Median r stays -9 to -12% either way.
Conclusion: volatility/ATRP base carries the whole REACH edge; known catalysts (8-K, insider, sector macro, runway) add <= a few pp and no reliable AUC. Untested: dated forward catalysts (ClinicalTrials/PDUFA), award size / market cap.

## 470 (2026-11-24) DEAL lead split by mechanism (financing vs commercial) and size, inside the high-vol group - NO PASS
246 DEAL flips (8-K 1.01/2.01, 7d), main doc text pulled from EDGAR, classified by earliest keyword: FIN 155, COM 85, OTH 6. Inside base group (top 30%): px<20 COM n36 REACH 67% v 60% no-DEAL (z+0.8), FIN n81 67% (z+1.2); px<10 COM n28 64% v 63% (z+0.1), FIN n48 73% (z+1.4, REAL 35% v 25% z+1.7); px<5 COM n12 83% v 70% (z+1.0, REAL 50% v 28% z+1.7, median r +8.7%), FIN n23 74% (z+0.4).
- Prediction (COM leads, FIN does not) NOT supported: FIN is as good or better at <20 and <10. COM alone is not a reliable lead (z <= 1.0 on REACH; era signs mixed, 2022 n 2-5). BIGDEAL (COM, size >= 10% mcap, first-$ parse is noisy: par values, tiny figures) n 8-19, z<=0.7.
- The only visible tilt is COM at <5 on REAL (+22pp, n12, z1.7) - 12 trades, not a finding.
- Reading: a DEAL 8-K of any kind is a small attention catalyst (+3-10pp REACH) and the financing/commercial label doesn't separate it. Size needs a proper amount parser (deal value tag, not first $ in text) before it can be judged.

## 471 (2026-11-24) forward clinical date (ClinicalTrials.gov Phase 2/3 primary completion in next 35d) - NO LEAD
89 of 172 health tickers matched to >=1 dated Phase 2/3 trial (1,319 trials). Current-record dates (history endpoint blocked) so completed trials carry actual dates - leak-prone, with BACK35/FAR placebos. Inside the base group (matched names): px<20 FWD35 n35 REACH 63% v 63% (z 0.0), P3 n14 64% v 63%, REAL 20% v 22%; px<10 n21 62% v 70% (z -0.8); px<5 n8. Placebos BACK35 54% v 64%, FAR 59% v 64% (no hindsight lift either). Nothing; n too small to rule out a modest effect in px<5.
## 472 (2026-11-24) FINRA short-sale volume and insider buying INSIDE the high-vol group
- FINRA SVR / change vs 20d / prior-5d / z60: AUC .48-.52 in every bucket, no PASS; quartile REACH flat (px<20 test 61/57/63/61%). Closed (also failed all-flips in 67b/FINRA family).
- Insider open-market P: AUC rule no PASS (.50-.52), but the FLAG rule passes for INS90 (any P filed in the prior 90d): px<20 REACH 66% v 59% no-insider (z +3.0), eras 66/62/66 v 59/60/59 (n 260/61/275); px<10 70% v 61% (z +2.6), eras 75/56/68 v 60/59/63; REAL 27% v 23% / 28% v 24%. px<5 74 v 69 (z 0.9). Median r unchanged (-9.8 v -9.5). INS30: 2022 reversal (48% v 60%, 44-36%) fails era rule; OFFICER REAL +10pp (z 2.3) pooled. CLUSTER and BIGBUY add nothing. Caveats: 1 of 6 flags x 3 buckets chosen after viewing (Bonferroni p~.02), +6-9pp REACH not a median fix, 2022 n only 27-61.

## 473 (2026-11-24) cut low-grade plays + EMA alignment on the live sub-$5 1h book - NO PASS (grade cut costs return)
Sub-$5 1h bull flips Dec 2023-Nov 2026, base = live E+V rules (skip <=3 sessions before earnings, skip >1 ATR above VWAP), 8 slots, 40bp, 5,725 flips with features (grade mix WEAK 59% / TOP 24% / STRONG 18%). Book C (vol>=1.5x + low-vol day, 652): base +85%/yr DD -19; grade>=STRONG (live default MIN_GRADE) +39%/yr DD -16, kept mean +4.19% v skipped +3.66%; TOP only +14%/yr, kept mean +2.44% v skipped +4.34% (TOP is WORSE than WEAK +3.66%); A200 +41%/yr (kept +3.49% v skipped +4.29%); STACK 20>50>200 n60 +8%/yr; RISE50 +22%/yr, kept +2.78% v +4.39%; A200&RISE50 +10%/yr; DAILYAL n99 +13%/yr. Book B (vol>=1.5x, 1700): base +112%/yr; grade>=STRONG +74%; TOP +46%; A200 +48%; RISE50 +54%; mean by grade TOP +3.16 / STRONG +3.65 / WEAK +2.93 (no ranking).
- 'Rejecting the EMA' idea: REJ = close below EMA50 after a high within 10 bars reached it. Flips with REJ lose the edge: C n35 mean +0.86% v +4.05%; B n104 +0.11% v +3.33% (p(rand) .30 / .12). Skipping them is roughly return-neutral (C +76 v +85%/yr, B +110 v +112%/yr, DD -22/-27 v -19/-22), too few to pass.
- Verdict: grade and EMA-trend alignment filters do not improve the book; they remove winners (sub-$5 winners come from beaten-down names below the EMAs). Live default MIN_GRADE=STRONG (skips WEAK) is not supported by this test: keeping WEAK is better. In-sample, thresholds untuned, 3 years.

## 474-476 (2026-11-24) wider price range (sub-$5/$10/$20) + EMA proximity + rejection stops + Bollinger on the live 1h book - NONE ADOPTED
Base = live E+V rules, book B (vol>=1.5x), Dec 2023-Nov 2026, 8 slots, 40bp.
- 474 (grade and EMA-trend filters over price buckets): the edge is a sub-$5 edge. Base ann/mean net per trade: sub-$5 +112% / +3.13% (1,700 trades), sub-$10 +41% / +1.66% (3,606), sub-$20 +33% / +0.91% (6,024). Grade>=STRONG (live default): +74 / +20 / +25% (all below base), TOP only +46 / +32 / +12, A200 +48 / +14 / +3, STACK 20>50>200 +21 / +5 / -15; mean by grade has no ranking (sub-$20: WEAK +1.03% > TOP +0.83% > STRONG +0.69%). 'Not REJ' (flip not just rejected at EMA50) skipped mean +0.1/+1.0/+0.4 vs kept +3.3/+1.7/+0.9 and p(rand) .12/.41/.34, return-neutral. DAILYAL (daily close>EMA20>EMA50) +38% vs +21% base in sub-$20 book C (p .095) but +7% vs +33% in book B: inconsistent.
- 475 (EMA proximity, 12 EMAs = 1h/4h/daily x 20/50/100/200; BE stop and exit after rejection): flips within +-1 ATR of an EMA are the WEAKEST bucket on most EMAs (sub-$5 1h50 NEAR +0.89% v ABOVE >1 ATR +3.77%; 4h50 NEAR +2.8% v BELOW<-1 +4.6%): the 'close enough to pass' zone is where flips chop, not where they pass. Stops mostly hurt (win rate falls to 16-22%, mean net +3.1% -> ~+2.0%). 1 lead of 72 looks: 4h20 exit-after-rejection beats base on ann/DD/H1/H2 in sub-$10 and sub-$20 but not sub-$5; neighbours (4h50, 1h20) do not agree -> chance.
- 476 (Bollinger 20,2 on 1h/4h/daily): %B at flip not monotone across timeframes (1h: >1 best +4.2%/+2.0%/+1.1%; daily: >1 worst +1.1%/+0.1%/0.0%, .75-1 best +5.5%/+2.9%/+1.3%); squeeze (bottom-third bandwidth) is NOT better than rest (4h sub-$5 +2.41% v +3.87%; daily +2.33% v +4.09%); upper-band rejection BE/exit stops all lose (1h exit -12/-31/-42% ann). Only 4h exit-on-close-below-basis in sub-$10 beats base on all four (1 of 27 looks).
Verdict: price-structure filters/stops (grade, EMA alignment/proximity, EMA/Bollinger rejection) add nothing over the vector exit; the vector flip already contains the trend information. The book's edge is concentrated in sub-$5 and shrinks per trade as price rises.

## 477 (2026-11-24) high-ATRP group on BOTH the daily and the 1h vector, with 'sell at +10% else ride the vector exit'
Daily = structure_small flips test 2022+ (base5 score, train-ECDF), 1h = live book flips Apr25-Nov26 (score = ATRP, flip-bar volume ratio, -LDV; cuts from Dec23-Mar25; E+V base). Costs 30bp daily / 40bp 1h, +10% fill assumed exact.
- DAILY REACH: sub-$20 top30% 60% / top10% 67%; sub-$10 63% / 69%; sub-$5 68% / 75% (median 3-5 days to +10%). 1H REACH: sub-$20 37% / 43%; sub-$10 40% / 46%; sub-$5 44% / 52% (median 2-4 days) - the 1h vector is a much weaker 'catch the move' selector than the daily.
- SELL +10% ELSE VECTOR EXIT: win rate = REACH (60-75% daily, 39-52% 1h) but mean per trade only +0.2% to +0.9% daily and +0.3% to +1.7% 1h, because the non-reachers ride to the vector exit and lose ~-12% to -18% on average (e.g. daily sub-$10 top10%: 69% win x +9.7% + 31% x ~-17%). Median +9.7% (daily) but the mean is ~0: the high hit rate is a payoff illusion without a loser rule.
- VECTOR EXIT ONLY (let winners run): win 28-32% daily, median -10% to -17%, mean +5% to +34% (sub-$5 top10% +33.8%, n91 over 4.4y, outlier-driven); 1h sub-$5 top10% (n119, H2 only) mean +17.6% win 41% median -3.1%.
- Hold: sell-at-+10% averages 8-10 days daily (4-5 days 1h) vs ~50-60 days (daily) / 11-14 days (1h) to the vector exit.
Takeaway: touching +10% is 2/3 likely in the daily top group, but capping at +10% leaves ~0 expectancy; the money is in the minority that runs, and the cost is a 70% loss rate on the vector-exit book. A signal-based loser exit that cuts the -12/-18% tail without cutting winners has not been found (EMA/Bollinger/grade stops failed in 473-476).

## 478 (2026-11-25) exit at the CLOSE of the bar that flips the daily vector bear vs the next open
Daily high-ATRP group, test 2022+, entries next open, 30bp. Close-exit (A open vs B close): win/mean/median essentially identical in every bucket (sub-$20 top10%: mean +8.9% / +8.9%, median -12.0% / -12.2%; sub-$10 top10% +18.3% / +17.9%; sub-$5 top10% +33.8% / +33.3%); p10 tail -22% to -31% either way. The vector level sits ~10-12% under entry on these names, so a close below it is already a ~-11% loss at the close; the fill (open vs close) is not the problem. Last-10 daily flips (charts): LCID/ASPN/BFLY flipped bear within 2 sessions (-12/-10/-11% at that close, -13% at the next open), BRBR +50% (peak +58%), ZIP -9%, ANGX -6%, BGS +4% still open.

## 479-480 (2026-11-25) 4h/1h close below the vector level as the exit; extension of the flip close above the vector level
- 479 (daily vector flips on the mid/small 1h universe, Dec 2023-Nov 2026, 3,105 flips / 541 names, 30bp): A = daily bear flip -> next open: win 21% mean +2.9% median -5.9% p10 -16% hold 37.6d; B4 = exit at the first 4h close below the level: win 19% mean +2.6% median -4.7% p10 -14% hold 27.6d; B1 (1h close): win 18% mean +2.6% median -4.3% hold 25.0d. Top-third ATRP (1,035): A mean +11.2% / median -9.6% / hold 44d vs B4 +9.6% / -7.9% / 32d vs B1 +9.6% / -7.1% / 30d. Big winners (A>=+30%: 241 / 143 in top third) are cut early by B4 in 238 / 140 cases (return +94% vs +115%; top third +122% vs +147%). Earlier exits trim the loser tail by ~1-2pts but give up 1.6pts of mean and ~20-25pts on each big winner; 'cut' counts are inflated because the same-day 4h/1h close precedes the next-open fill.
- 480 (daily flips, base5 top 30%, test 2022+, quartiles of EXT_L = (close - daily vector level)/close cut on train): the MORE extended the flip close, the better the REACH and mean but the worse the loss: sub-$20 REACH 47/54/64/72%, mean +2.5/+5.2/+3.0/+10.1%, median -7.7/-10.1/-12.0/-15.0%, p10 -14/-16/-19/-28% (Q1..Q4, EXT_L 5% -> 19%); sub-$10 REACH 50/60/66/73%, p10 -15/-17/-22/-31%; sub-$5 REACH 57/72/64/77%, p10 -17/-17/-25/-30%. Extension = thrust (more big winners) AND a larger built-in loss (~ EXT_L) when it fails. Skipping extended flips would drop the winners; EXT_L is a risk-sizing input (size ~ 1/EXT_L), not a filter.
- Catalyst audit of the last 15 signals (SEC filings + news): 9 of 15 had an earnings report within ~7 days before or just after the flip; none had a deal 8-K (1.01), 13D or offering except an ASPN S-3 shelf and ALXO mgmt-change 8-K; Form 4s on most (buy vs sell not distinguishable from the filing list; insider-P history ends Mar 2026). BRBR (+58%) and FRMI (+63%) peaks came on earnings released AFTER entry.
- Report 482 (overreaction / bottom), daily top-30% base5 group, test 2022+: no obvious bottom marker. Fresh low (LOWAGE<=3) is NOT better than older (sub-$20 mean +3.4% vs +5.9%); volume-climax capitulation (drop<=-20%, unexplained, climax>=3x, low within 5d) is worse: n 37/26/13, mean -3.7/-0.3/+0.3%, win 15-19%. Deeper pre-flip drop = higher REACH and mean (sub-$10 Q1 +19.2% vs Q4 +6.6%) but median stays -10 to -14% and 2022 is flat/negative (Q1 2022 -0.3% to -5.1%). Overreaction proxy = deep drop (<=-20%) with NO earnings/bad 8-K in the 20d window beats explained drops: sub-$5 +47.0% vs +5.8%, sub-$10 +24.2% vs +6.1%, sub-$20 +12.5% vs +6.3%; 2022 sign positive but tiny n (14/34/69), edge is 2023-26. Verdict: lead, not a pass (capitulation set fails the declared rule); candidate rating/size input, not a filter; does not fix the negative median.
- Report 483 (ATR-relative drop gate, rank vs cut), daily top-30% group, test 2022+: DROPX = 20d drawdown / ATRP, gate = train top tercile (~3+ ATRs). The report-482 'unexplained deep drop' lead does NOT survive: top-DROPX tercile UNEXPL vs EXPL = sub-$20 -1.6% vs +6.1%, sub-$10 +4.5% vs +7.7%, sub-$5 +11.6% vs +5.5%; month-clustered spread t -1.62 / +0.16 / +0.25, 2022 alone ~0. DROPX is non-monotone (best = middle tercile ~2.7 ATRs: sub-$5 +35.6%, sub-$10 +16.7%). CUT explained-deep and RANK 2/1/.5 vs ALL: paired t -0.44/-1.34 (sub-$20), +0.06/-0.07 (sub-$10), +0.29/+0.38 (sub-$5). Read: the fixed -20% gate was mostly selecting high-ATRP names, not overreaction. Verdict: FAIL; no cut, no rank. Overreaction lead closed.
- Report 484 (feature ablation of base5), daily flips, test 2022+, top-30% groups: the 5 features are mostly redundant (all proxy 'small, volatile, illiquid-ish'). Sub-$5: base5 mean +16.8%/REACH 68% vs ATRP-only +15.4%/66%, F11-only +15.2%/68%, LTURN-only +17.3%/66%, VOLX-only +10.7%, LMC-only +13.1%; drop-one versions +15.0 to +18.9% (no feature is essential). Sub-$10: base5 +9.7%/63% vs ATRP-only +9.3%/61%, F11-only +8.1%/65%. Sub-$20: base5 +5.5%/60% vs ATRP-only +5.9%/60%. Month-clustered base5 minus best single: t -1.33 / -0.27 / -0.50 (fails 'combination beats best single'). Verdict: combination is NOT better than ATRP (or LTURN at sub-$5); it is a more stable, lower-variance way to get the same group. VOLX is the weakest single; dropping it never hurts.
- Report 485 (EMA vs VWAP for the daily-flip chart), base5 top-30% groups, selection on train 2019-21, test 2022+: nothing to select. At a daily flip close price is ABOVE the fast averages (EMA 8/13/21, VWAP 5/10) in 95-99% of flips (below-side n 2-23), so they cannot split anything; no EMA or VWAP had a positive train gap on mean AND REACH in sub-$10/sub-$5 (sub-$20 picked V20, test gap -4.3% t -0.49). Slow averages (EMA 50/100/200, VWAP 50/100): train gaps negative in all buckets (above is worse); post-hoc test confirms only weakly (sub-$20 E100 gap -6.6% t -1.84; sub-$5 E100 -13.5% t -1.26; E200 ~0 everywhere; V100 mixed sign). Session VWAP resets daily so carries nothing at a daily close. Verdict: no EMA/VWAP passes; none is a filter. For the chart keep the daily vector level + a slow reference (EMA 100/200) for context only; drop session VWAP.
- Report 486 (upside-jump vs chop volatility inside the ATRP top-30% group), test 2022+: nothing passes. UPS (share of 60d variance from up days) is NOT better when high (gap -0.9%/-8.8%/-22.0% sub-$20/$10/$5, t<=-0.13..-0.92; the LOW-UPS tail is a tiny n41 outlier); MAXR (largest 1-day gain) lifts REACH (54->65%, 57->68%, 62->78%) but not the mean (t +0.46/-0.16/+0.09); NJ (count of +15% days) top group mean +25.5%/+18.4%/+56.9% on n 121/46/14, t +0.95/+0.64/+1.57, 2022 flat/negative at sub-$20 (-3.3%) and positive at sub-$5 (+81.5% n tiny); DNJ control behaves the same way as NJ at sub-$20 (+30.6%, n79), i.e. it is a 'jumpy' flag, not an upside flag. Together with reports 466/467 (GK22, HAR, VOV, GAPABS20, SURP) the volatility direction is saturated: everything is the same information as ATRP, and REACH tops out ~68-70% whatever the score. Lead only: jump COUNT (NJ/MAXR) raises REACH; not adopted.
- Nov 25 2026: VOLX (flip-bar volume ratio) dropped from the live sub-$5 daily alert score (report 484: weakest single feature; without it train AND test are >= base5 at sub-$10 (test +10.2% vs +9.7%, REACH 65% vs 63%) and sub-$5 (+18.9% vs +16.8%, REACH 68% = 68%), tie at sub-$20 (+4.9% vs +5.5%)). Live score = mean ECDF rank of ATRP, F11, LTURN, -LMC; frozen train cuts rebuilt in daily_base5_ref.json (xs/build_daily_ref.py). Volume ratio is still shown in the alert as information.
- Report 487 (follow-through / signal exits on the 4-feature group, daily, test 2022+, entry next open, exit next open after the signal close, 30bp; sim BASE reproduces structure_small r exactly: sub-$5 +18.9%, sub-$10 +10.2%): NO exit rule beats the vector exit. Sub-$5 (n 295) mean vs BASE +18.9%: X1 close<flip-day low +15.2% (t -1.75; keeps 83% of +30% winners), X2 close<entry-1.5xATRP +12.5% (t -2.15, 74%), X3 distribution down-close before +10% +16.0% (t -1.76, 89%), X4 lock-after-+10% close<prior low +2.6% (keeps 19% of +30% winners), X1+X4 +1.5%. Sub-$10 (n 790): -1.9/-2.2/-0.5/-4.7/-4.9 points, t -2.00/-1.87/-0.74/-1.84/-1.86. They raise the median (-11.4 -> -10.6 / +0.9 for X4) and lift p10 slightly but cost 2-9 points of mean by cutting the big winners (2023-26 hit hardest; 2022 slightly helped by X1/X3). The payoff is the minority of runners; any early signal that cuts losers also cuts runners. Verdict: FAIL; keep the vector exit.
- Literature hints (abstract-level only): Bali-Cakici-Whitelaw 'Maxing Out' (NBER 14804): high past-month MAX predicts LOWER next-month returns (lottery preference, and including MAX flips the IVOL puzzle); different horizon/object from our flips (weeks, REACH) but consistent with our finding that MAXR lifts REACH without lifting the mean after the vector exit. Chan 2003 (summary only): bad-news stocks drift down up to 12 months, no-news big moves reverse next month, both stronger in small illiquid names = the report-482/483 overreaction idea, which failed our ATR-relative test. Gu et al. 2016 (empfin 37): news arrivals explain about half of the IVOL-return relation, and the relation disappears for value-relevant firm-specific news.
- Report 488 (cherry-pick flips after GOOD catalysts, 4-feature group = live alert group, test 2022+, flags known at flip close): no good-catalyst flag passes (flag beats rest on mean AND REACH in both eras with t>=2.2). Sub-$20 (n 1593): EARNP (earnings 8-K with 2d reaction>=+5%) n320 +5.0% vs +4.9% (t -1.74); DEAL n79 +8.5% (t +0.80); INS n142 +9.5% vs +4.5% (t +0.99, REACH 65 vs 60, 2022 +3.0 vs -1.8); ACT (13D) n105 +6.1% (t -0.14); SPIKE n82 +3.9%, median -13.6%; GOODANY n618 +6.0% vs +4.3% (t +0.30). Sub-$10 (n 790): GOODANY n321 +10.3% vs +10.2% (REACH 68 vs 62); INS n72 +13.4% vs +9.9%; ACT n55 +16.9% (2022 n9 -7%). Sub-$5 (n 295): GOODANY n126 +10.8% vs REST +24.9% (t -1.29), REACH 72 vs 65: good-catalyst flips reach +10% a bit more often but do not average more; unexplained flips do as well or better. Control NEGBAD (3.01/1.02/1.03/2.05/2.06/4.02) is the only flag that separates: -8.0%/-6.2%/-8.6% means (t -2.77 at sub-$20; n 28/20/11), REACH 46% at sub-$20. Verdict: positive catalysts do not explain the winners; INS = lead only (consistent sign sub-$20/$10, small n); bad-catalyst warning stays a warning.
